Original Abstract: Diffusion-based generative video compression has emerged as a promising paradigm to improve perceptual quality, where latent frames are required to be encoded efficiently while serving as denoising conditions. However, existing methods neither carefully design reference and quality structures during latent coding nor account for the impact of frame-level quality variation on denoising procedure, which limits coding efficiency and aggravates artifact propagation during generative reconstruction. In this paper, we propose GVCHR, Generative Video Compression based on Hierarchical Referencing. The key idea is to organize latent frames hierarchically, where the selected high-quality references benefit both latent coding and generative reconstruction. In latent coding, GVCHR couples a hierarchical reference structure with a hierarchical quality structure, assigning more bits to lower-layer frames that are reused more frequently as references. Built on this design, we introduce Hierarchical Temporal Context Mining to exploits complementary short- and long-term temporal context for effective latent coding. In generative reconstruction, the coding-side hierarchy is incorporated into a Hierarchical Attentive Adapter which is attached to a video diffusion transformer. This adapter uses hierarchical attention to restrict each latent frame to attend only to the same- or lower-layer references, thereby reducing artifact propagation during denoising. Experiments validate GVCHR on multiple benchmarks. Compared with the previous state-of-the-art method, GVCHR achieves 50.5% and 54.0% BD-rate gains in terms of LPIPS and DISTS, respectively, while also delivering clearly improved visual quality.

中文摘要翻译: 基于扩散的生成式视频压缩已成为提升感知质量的有前景范式，其中潜帧需被高效编码并同时作为去噪条件。然而，现有方法在潜编码阶段既未精心设计引用结构与质量结构，也未考虑帧级质量变化对去噪过程的影响，这限制了编码效率并在生成重建过程中加剧了伪影传播。本文提出GVCHR（基于层级引用的生成式视频压缩）。其核心思想是将潜帧组织为层级结构，其中选定的高质量引用对潜编码和生成重建均有裨益。在潜编码中，GVCHR将层级引用结构与层级质量结构相耦合，为更底层且被更频繁复用的帧分配更多比特。在此基础上，我们引入层级时序上下文挖掘，以利用互补的短期和长期时域上下文实现高效潜编码。在生成重建中，编码端层级被融入附加于视频扩散Transformer的层级注意力适配器中。该适配器利用层级注意力限制每个潜帧仅关注同层或更低层引用，从而减少去噪过程中的伪影传播。实验在多个基准上验证了GVCHR。与先前最先进方法相比，GVCHR在LPIPS和DISTS指标上分别取得50.5%和54.0%的BD-rate增益，同时带来明显改善的视觉质量。

</details>


### [29] [CAM-Guided Saliency Cutout and Image-Based Malware Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11634)
*Yasaman Ebrahimi,Martin Jurecek,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了是否可以利用HiResCAM（高分辨率类别激活映射）来改进Cutout（图像裁剪）的位置选择，并在恶意软件灰度图像（RawMal-TF数据集）和自然图像（CIFAR-100）上进行了对照实验，结果发现基于显著性的cutout效果具有领域依赖性，恶意软件图像不应被等同于自然图像处理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Cutout作为一种类似于Dropout的正则化技术，通常随机选择裁剪区域。作者推测通过HiResCAM生成的高分辨率类别激活图可以指导裁剪位置（高显著性或低显著性区域），从而可能提升正则化效果。这一假设需要实验验证，尤其是在恶意软件图像这一特殊领域。

Method: 对比四种受控训练条件：无cutout、标准随机cutout、低显著性cutout和高显著性cutout。使用ResNet18作为骨干网络，训练约100个epoch。测试了cutout面积（约5%、10%、20%、30%）和每张原始训练图像的增强副本数M∈{4,8}。实验在两个数据集上进行：RawMal-TF（17个家族的灰度恶意软件图像，每家族约1000个样本）和CIFAR-100（自然图像）。

Result: 在RawMal-TF数据集上，三种cutout场景（随机、高显著性和低显著性）的结果均略差于无cutout。而在CIFAR-100数据集上，低显著性cutout的结果略有改善。这表明saliency-guided cutout的效果依赖于数据领域。

Conclusion: 基于显著性的cutout裁剪在恶意软件图像上不能带来性能提升，甚至在所有cutout变体下都略逊于无cutout基线；而在自然图像上低显著性cutout略有帮助。结论是saliency-guided cutout的价值具有领域依赖性，恶意软件图像不应被视为与自然图像等价。

Original Abstract: Dropout regularization is commonly used to reduce overfitting by removing parts of a neural network during training. For Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN), cutouts serve a somewhat analogous purpose. Cutouts can be implemented as data augmentation: the original training image is retained, and additional copies are created with regions removed. In this chapter, we test whether cutout placement can be improved by using High-Resolution Class Activation Mapping (HiResCAM). We compare four controlled training conditions: no cutout, standard random cutout, low-saliency cutout, and high-saliency cutout. We experiment using grayscale malware images from the RawMal-TF dataset (17 families with~1,000 samples per family), and for comparison to natural images, we experiment with the well-known CIFAR-100 dataset. All experiments are based on ResNet18 with~100 training epochs. For the cutout experiments, we test cutout areas of~5\%, 10\%, 20\%, and~30\%, and we consider~$M\in\{4,8}$ augmented copies per original training image. The RawMal-TF results are slightly worse for all three cutout cases (random, high and low saliency) as compared to no cutouts. In contrast, our CIFAR-100 experimental results improve slightly under low-saliency cutout. These results suggest that the value of saliency-guided cutout is domain dependent, and that malware images should not be treated as equivalent to natural images.

中文摘要翻译: Dropout正则化通常通过在训练过程中移除神经网络的部分单元来减少过拟合。对于卷积神经网络（CNN），cutout在一定程度上起到类似的作用。Cutout可以作为一种数据增强方式实现：保留原始训练图像，并创建带有区域移除的额外副本。在本章中，我们测试是否可以通过使用高分辨率类别激活映射（HiResCAM）来改进cutout的放置位置。我们比较了四种受控训练条件：无cutout、标准随机cutout、低显著性cutout和高显著性cutout。我们使用来自RawMal-TF数据集的灰度恶意软件图像进行实验（17个家族，每个家族约1,000个样本），并与自然图像进行比较，使用了著名的CIFAR-100数据集。所有实验均基于ResNet18，训练约100个epoch。对于cutout实验，我们测试了约5%、10%、20%和30%的cutout面积，并考虑每张原始训练图像M∈{4,8}个增强副本。在RawMal-TF数据集上，三种cutout情况（随机、高显著性和低显著性）的结果均略差于无cutout。相比之下，我们的CIFAR-100实验结果在低显著性cutout下略有改善。这些结果表明，显著性引导的cutout的价值具有领域依赖性，恶意软件图像不应被视作等同于自然图像。

</details>


### [30] [Robustness of AI-Art Detectors under Generator Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11643)
*Shivank Singh Thakur,Meien Li,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了文本到图像生成模型（特别是Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium）带来的"生成器偏移"问题，分析了现有AI艺术检测器在跨生成器零样本设置下的鲁棒性。研究发现基于U-Net架构训练的检测器在生成器偏移下性能显著下降，将大量SD3.5m图像误分类为人类作品，其中CLIP ViT-L/14表现最佳，Grad-CAM分析揭示了误分类样本上激活更弱且更弥散的现象，凸显了当前检测器的泛化差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现代Diffusion Transformer架构生成的图像与人类艺术作品越来越难以区分，引发了版权保护、虚假信息、欺诈、身份冒用和数字内容真实性等严重担忧。然而大多数AI艺术检测器在同一生成器家族上训练和评估，对更新架构的鲁棒性研究不足。作者旨在探究跨生成器场景下检测器的泛化能力，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 基于Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium（SD3.5m）构建涵盖十种艺术风格的数据集，通过反向提示（reverse prompting）生成持有的人类艺术作品样本。训练五个基于U-Net潜在扩散艺术品数据的检测器，并在零样本跨生成器设置下对SD3.5m数据集进行评估，同时采用Grad-CAM进行可解释性分析。

Result: 深度学习模型在分布内表现强劲，但在生成器偏移下性能显著退化，将大量SD3.5m图像误分类为人类作品，而人类假阳性率保持较低。CLIP ViT-L/14模型整体表现最佳。Grad-CAM分析显示，被误分类（假阴性）的样本上激活更弱且更弥散。

Conclusion: 当前AI艺术检测器存在显著的泛化差距，无法在快速演进的生成架构下保持可靠性能。检测器应作为分层防御体系中的一个组成部分，以在跨生成器场景下维持可靠性。

Original Abstract: Text-to-image generative models have advanced rapidly, with modern Diffusion Transformer architectures producing images that are increasingly difficult to distinguish from human-created artwork. This development has raised significant concerns regarding copyright protection, misinformation, fraud, impersonation, and the authenticity of digital content. Most AI-art detectors are trained and evaluated on the same generator family, leaving robustness to newer architectures underexplored. In this chapter, we analyze generator shift based on a Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium (SD3.5m) artwork dataset spanning ten art styles through reverse prompting of held-out human artwork samples. Five detectors are trained on U-Net-based latent diffusion artwork and evaluated in a zero-shot cross-generator setting on the SD3.5m dataset. Deep learning models perform strongly in-distribution but degrade under generator shift, misclassifying many SD3.5m images as human while human false positives remain low. The CLIP ViT-L/14 model performs best overall, while Grad-CAM analysis reveals weaker and more diffuse activation on false negatives. These findings highlight a generalization gap in current AI-art detectors and motivate the development of detectors as one component of a layered defense that remains reliable across rapidly evolving generative architectures.

中文摘要翻译: 文本到图像生成模型发展迅速，现代扩散Transformer架构生成的图像越来越难以与人类创作的艺术作品区分。这一发展引发了关于版权保护、虚假信息、欺诈、身份冒用以及数字内容真实性的重大关切。大多数AI艺术检测器在同一生成器家族上训练和评估，导致其对更新架构的鲁棒性尚未得到充分探索。在本章节中，我们基于Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium（SD3.5m）艺术品数据集（涵盖十种艺术风格），通过对持有的人类艺术作品样本进行反向提示来分析生成器偏移。五个检测器在基于U-Net的潜在扩散艺术品上训练，并在零样本跨生成器设置下于SD3.5m数据集上评估。深度学习模型在分布内表现强劲，但在生成器偏移下性能退化，将大量SD3.5m图像误分类为人类作品，而人类假阳性率保持较低。CLIP ViT-L/14模型整体表现最佳，Grad-CAM分析揭示了假阴性样本上的激活更弱且更弥散。这些发现凸显了当前AI艺术检测器的泛化差距，并激励将检测器发展为分层防御体系中的一个组成部分，以便在快速演进的生成架构中保持可靠性。

</details>


### [31] [Cloak of Invisibility: Real-Time Privacy-Preserving Volumetric Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11645)
*Hossein Khalili,Philip Do,Alexander Vilesov,Kittipat Apicharttrisorn,Nader Sehatbakhsh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InViStream，一个面向体视频流媒体的实时"源头隐私保护"系统。它解决了多视角RGB-D体视频采集中的隐私泄漏问题——私有对象在融合前必须从源端移除，而公共场景仍需保持可用于实时重建。系统结合目标检测与深度感知掩码、跨标定视角传播公共/私有决策，并仅融合消毒后的点云。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 体视频流媒体将隐私问题扩展到三维多视角场景：不同于普通视频可逐帧删除敏感内容，RGB-D体视频管道从多个摄像机捕获人物、房间和个人物品并融合为共享3D表示。某一视角遗漏或融合前未完全移除的私有对象可能在重建场景中重新出现。现有体视频流媒体系统主要优化重建、数据传输和延迟，而隐私保护视觉方法针对单相机单帧图像设计，无法直接处理标定多视角RGB-D融合问题。

Method: InViStream解决了三个核心挑战：(1) 私有对象在不同视角下呈现差异，采用目标检测结合深度感知掩码（depth-aware masking）处理RGB和深度双通道的隐私泄漏；(2) 在标定多视角间传播公共/私有决策，确保跨视角一致性；(3) 仅融合经过消毒处理（sanitized）的点云，防止私有几何信息在云侧重建中重现。

Result: 在合成和真实RGB-D场景（包括办公室、会议室、客厅以及多公共/私有人员和物体的场景）上评估：合成数据Dice/Recall为0.799/0.891，真实数据Dice/Recall为0.792/0.908；合成SSIM高于0.98；实时流媒体速度超过30 FPS。

Conclusion: InViStream通过从源头进行隐私消除（privacy-from-source）的方式，在体视频实时流媒体中实现了有效的隐私保护，同时保持了公共场景的可用性和实时重建性能，为3D远程临场、教育、娱乐等沉浸式应用提供了可行的隐私安全方案。

Original Abstract: Volumetric video streaming turns privacy into a 3D, multi-view problem. Unlike ordinary video, where sensitive content can often be redacted frame by frame, RGB-D volumetric pipelines capture people, rooms, and personal objects from multiple cameras and fuse them into a shared 3D representation. A private object missed in one view, or only partially removed before fusion, can therefore reappear in the reconstructed scene. This creates a privacy challenge for 3D telepresence, education, entertainment, and immersive applications: private content should be removed before raw visual and geometric data leave the camera side, while the public part of the scene should remain useful for real-time reconstruction. Existing volumetric streaming systems mainly optimize reconstruction, data movement, and latency, while privacy-preserving vision methods are designed for single-camera, single-frame images and do not directly address calibrated multi-view RGB-D fusion. We present InViStream, a real-time "privacy-from-source" system designed for this setting. InViStream addresses three challenges in volumetric capture: private objects may appear differently across views, RGB masking alone can leave geometric privacy leakage in depth, and public/private instances of the same class must be separated consistently before cloud-side fusion. To address these challenges, InViStream combines object detection with depth-aware masking, propagates public/private decisions across calibrated views, and fuses only sanitized point clouds. We evaluate InViStream on synthetic and real RGB-D scenes, including offices, conference rooms, living rooms, and settings with multiple public and private people and objects. InViStream achieves synthetic Dice/Recall of 0.799/0.891 and real Dice/Recall of 0.792/0.908, with synthetic SSIM above 0.98 and real-time streaming above 30 FPS.

中文摘要翻译: 体视频流媒体将隐私问题转化为一个三维多视角问题。与普通视频（敏感内容通常可以逐帧删除）不同，RGB-D体视频管道从多个摄像机捕获人物、房间和个人物品，并将它们融合为共享的三维表示。某个视角遗漏的私有对象，或融合前仅被部分移除的对象，可能在重建场景中重新出现。这为3D远程临场、教育、娱乐和沉浸式应用带来了一项隐私挑战：私有内容应在原始视觉和几何数据离开摄像机端之前被移除，而场景的公共部分应保持对实时重建有用。现有的体视频流媒体系统主要优化重建、数据传输和延迟，而隐私保护的视觉方法是为单摄像机、单帧图像设计的，不能直接处理标定的多视角RGB-D融合。我们提出InViStream，一个为这一场景设计的实时"源头隐私保护"系统。InViStream应对体视频捕获中的三个挑战：私有对象在不同视角下可能呈现不同外观；仅RGB掩码可能留下深度通道中的几何隐私泄漏；同一类别的公共/私有实例必须在云侧融合前被一致地分离。为应对这些挑战，InViStream将目标检测与深度感知掩码相结合，跨标定视角传播公共/私有决策，并仅融合消毒处理后的点云。我们在合成和真实RGB-D场景上评估InViStream，包括办公室、会议室、客厅以及包含多个公共和私有人物与物体的场景。InViStream在合成数据上达到Dice/Recall为0.799/0.891，在真实数据上达到Dice/Recall为0.792/0.908，合成SSIM高于0.98，实时流媒体速度超过30 FPS。

</details>


### [32] [Motion-as-Prompt: Enhancing Motion Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models via Motion-Guided Cross-Frame Visual Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11655)
*Xikai Sun,Kebin Liu,Haotian Wang,Li Liu,Xu Wang,Yunhao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Motion-as-Prompt (MaP)，一种基于轨迹引导的跨帧视觉提示框架，通过恢复密集点轨迹、选择运动信息丰富的帧并将其直接标记到视觉输入上，使冻结的多模态大语言模型能够感知帧间隐藏的位移、方向变化和交互信息，从而在不进行模型训练或架构修改的情况下提升运动中心视频推理能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态大语言模型通常通过稀疏均匀采样来处理视频以控制视觉token和注意力成本，但这一策略会丢弃采样帧之间的关键状态转换，从而限制模型对物体运动、碰撞和因果交互的推理能力。

Method: MaP框架包含三个步骤：首先恢复密集点轨迹，其次选择运动信息丰富的帧，最后在连续采样帧之间将累积的轨迹直接标记到视觉输入上，使原本隐藏的位移、方向变化和交互对冻结的MLLM可见。该方法无需模型训练或架构修改。

Result: 在CLEVRER和Something-Something-v2数据集上，MaP分别将GPT-5.5的平均运动推理准确率提升了4.2%和8.9%，且不影响非运动理解能力，证明了其鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MaP为增强运动中心视频推理提供了一种简单而有效的解决方案，无需模型训练或架构修改，且不损害非运动理解能力。

Original Abstract: Motion-centric video reasoning is fundamental to interactive applications such as robotic manipulation and autonomous navigation. However, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) typically process videos through sparse uniform sampling to control visual-token and attention costs. This strategy may discard critical transitions between sampled frames, limiting reasoning about object movement, collisions, and causal interactions. To mitigate this issue, we propose Motion-as-Prompt (MaP), a track-guided cross-frame visual prompting framework. MaP recovers dense point trajectories, selects motion-informative frames, and marks the trajectories accumulated between consecutive sampled frames directly onto the visual inputs, making otherwise hidden displacement, direction changes, and interactions observable to frozen MLLMs. Experiments on CLEVRER and Something-Something-v2 show that MaP consistently improves average motion-reasoning accuracy, yielding gains of 4.2% and 8.9% for GPT-5.5, respectively. Notably, these improvements are obtained without degrading non-motion understanding, highlighting the robustness of MaP. These results demonstrate that MaP provides a simple and effective solution for enhancing motion-centric video reasoning without model training or architectural modification. Project page:https://github.com/SunVictor23/MaP.

中文摘要翻译: 以运动为中心的视频推理是机器人操作和自主导航等交互式应用的基础。然而，多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通常通过稀疏均匀采样来处理视频，以控制视觉token和注意力成本。这种策略可能会丢弃采样帧之间的关键状态转换，从而限制对物体运动、碰撞和因果交互的推理。为解决这一问题，我们提出了Motion-as-Prompt (MaP)，一种轨迹引导的跨帧视觉提示框架。MaP恢复密集点轨迹，选择运动信息丰富的帧，并将连续采样帧之间累积的轨迹直接标记到视觉输入上，使原本隐藏的位移、方向变化和交互对冻结的MLLM可见。在CLEVRER和Something-Something-v2上的实验表明，MaP持续提升平均运动推理准确率，分别对GPT-5.5带来4.2%和8.9%的提升。值得注意的是，这些改进在不降低非运动理解能力的情况下获得，突显了MaP的鲁棒性。这些结果表明，MaP为增强运动中心视频推理提供了一种简单有效的解决方案，无需模型训练或架构修改。项目页面：https://github.com/SunVictor23/MaP。

</details>


### [33] [Zero-OVCD: Bridging Training-Free Foundation Models and Pseudo-Label Learning for Open-Vocabulary Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11663)
*Daifeng Peng,Yuanke Peng,Haiyan Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Zero-OVCD，一个无需目标域像素级标注的两阶段框架，用于开放词汇变化检测（OVCD）。第一阶段通过互补候选掩膜精炼、多尺度语义相似度融合与基于边距的可靠性过滤、以及响应引导的掩膜校正与补全来生成高质量变化伪标签；第二阶段用伪标签训练变化检测器，并引入检查点投票和高一致性样本选择来减轻残留噪声。在LEVIR-CD、WHU-CD、S2Looking和SECOND数据集上均取得了显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的无训练（training-free）开放词汇变化检测流程存在三个关键问题：候选掩膜不准确、语义分配模糊、以及推理错误累积。此外，目标域像素级标注难以获取，需要一种无需目标域像素级注释的解决方案。

Method: Zero-OVCD是一个两阶段框架。第一阶段生成高质量变化伪标签，具体包括三个组成部分：(1) 互补候选掩膜精炼（complementary candidate-mask refinement），抑制噪声候选；(2) 多尺度语义相似度融合与基于边距的可靠性过滤（margin-based reliability filtering），增强掩膜级语义判别；(3) 响应引导的掩膜校正与补全（response-guided mask correction and completion），恢复漏检的变化区域。第二阶段使用生成的伪标签训练变化检测器，并引入检查点投票（checkpoint voting）和高一致性样本选择（high-agreement sample selection）来缓解残留伪标签噪声。

Result: 在LEVIR-CD、WHU-CD和S2Looking上，第一阶段F1分数分别达到86.25%、85.82%和50.48%，第二阶段进一步提升至88.65%、88.85%和57.96%。在SECOND数据集上，六个类别的一对多任务的宏平均F1分数从47.91%提升到50.92%。

Conclusion: 将无训练的基础模型推理与噪声感知的伪标签学习相结合，为无需目标域像素级标注的开放词汇变化检测提供了一种有效解决方案，实验结果在多个基准数据集上验证了该方法的有效性。

Original Abstract: Open-vocabulary change detection (OVCD) enables the identification of user-specified land-cover changes in bitemporal remote sensing images, but existing training-free pipelines remain vulnerable to inaccurate candidate masks, ambiguous semantic assignments, and accumulated inference errors. To address these issues, we propose Zero-OVCD, a two-stage framework that requires no pixel-level annotations from the target domain. In the first stage, high-quality change pseudo-labels are generated through complementary candidate-mask refinement, multiscale semantic similarity fusion with margin-based reliability filtering, and response-guided mask correction and completion. These components jointly suppress noisy candidates, enhance mask-level semantic discrimination, and recover missed change regions. In the second stage, a change detector is trained using the generated pseudo-labels, while checkpoint voting and high-agreement sample selection are introduced to mitigate residual pseudo-label noise. On LEVIR-CD, WHU-CD, and S2Looking, Stage I achieves F1 scores of 86.25%, 85.82%, and 50.48%, while Stage II further improves them to 88.65%, 88.85%, and 57.96%, respectively. On SECOND, the macro-average F1 across six category-wise one-vs-rest tasks increases from 47.91% to 50.92%. These results demonstrate that bridging training-free foundation-model inference with noise-aware pseudo-label learning provides an effective solution for open-vocabulary change detection without target-domain pixel-level annotations. Code will be available at https://github.com/1321663019/Zero-OVCD.

中文摘要翻译: 开放词汇变化检测（OVCD）能够在双时相遥感图像中识别用户指定的土地覆盖变化，但现有的无训练（training-free）流程仍然容易受到不准确的候选掩膜、模糊的语义分配以及累积推理错误的影响。为解决这些问题，我们提出了Zero-OVCD，一个无需目标域像素级标注的两阶段框架。在第一阶段，通过互补候选掩膜精炼、基于边距的可靠性过滤的多尺度语义相似度融合，以及响应引导的掩膜校正与补全，生成高质量的变化伪标签。这些组件协同抑制噪声候选，增强掩膜级语义判别能力，并恢复漏检的变化区域。在第二阶段，使用生成的伪标签训练变化检测器，同时引入检查点投票和高一致性样本选择以缓解残留的伪标签噪声。在LEVIR-CD、WHU-CD和S2Looking上，第一阶段F1分数分别达到86.25%、85.82%和50.48%，第二阶段进一步将其提升至88.65%、88.85%和57.96%。在SECOND上，六个类别的一对多任务的宏平均F1分数从47.91%提升至50.92%。这些结果表明，将无训练基础模型推理与噪声感知的伪标签学习相结合，为无需目标域像素级标注的开放词汇变化检测提供了一种有效的解决方案。代码将在https://github.com/1321663019/Zero-OVCD上公开。

</details>


### [34] [Learning from Multimodal Pseudo-Labels for Robust Open-Vocabulary Instance and Panoptic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11681)
*Duy Tran Thanh,Yeejin Lee,Byeongkeun Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多模态框架，利用预训练的视觉-语言模型（Grounded SAM、LLaVA、CLIP和GPT）为开放词汇实例分割（OVIS）和开放集全景分割（OSPS）自动生成伪标签、同义词过滤和描述性caption重建，并通过三种互补的训练目标增强视觉-文本对齐，在COCO数据集上显著超越现有最先进方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有开放词汇实例分割和开放集全景分割方法面临三大挑战：伪掩码噪声大、视觉-文本对齐不足、难以处理同义词和词表外（OOV）单词。同时，传统方法依赖详尽的人工标注，成本高昂且难以覆盖所有类别。

Method: 提出一个多模态框架，在目标词汇辅助伪标签设置下：(1) 利用Grounded SAM、LLaVA和CLIP自动构建伪分割掩码、描述性caption和语义对齐的同义词集，提供无需人工标注的多模态监督；(2) 通过三个互补的训练目标增强视觉-文本对齐：扩展的grounding损失（纳入视觉grounded同义词）、语义一致性损失和生成式caption重建损失。

Result: 在COCO数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在该协议下持续优于先前最先进的方法，在OVIS和OSPS基准上均取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: 利用大规模预训练视觉-语言模型自动生成多模态监督信号，并辅以同义词过滤和caption重建损失，能够有效解决开放词汇分割中的伪标签噪声和语义对齐不足问题，为无详尽人工标注的分割任务提供了一条可行路径。

Original Abstract: This work addresses the challenge of open-vocabulary instance segmentation (OVIS) and open-set panoptic segmentation (OSPS), which aim to recognize both predefined and unseen object categories without exhaustive human annotations. Existing methods often suffer from noisy pseudo-masks, limited visual-textual grounding, and difficulty handling synonyms or out-of-vocabulary (OOV) words. To overcome these challenges, we propose a multimodal framework that leverages pre-trained vision-language models for automatic pseudo-label generation, CLIP-guided synonym filtering, and GPT-based caption reconstruction. In our target-vocabulary-assisted pseudo-labeling setting, the framework first constructs pseudo segmentation masks, descriptive captions, and semantically aligned synonym sets using Grounded SAM, LLaVA, and CLIP, providing multimodal supervision without manual annotation. We then enhance visual-textual alignment through three complementary training objectives: an extended grounding loss that incorporates visually grounded synonyms, a semantic consistency loss, and a generative caption reconstruction loss. Extensive experiments on the COCO dataset demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art approaches under this protocol, achieving substantial improvements on both OVIS and OSPS benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 本文工作解决了开放词汇实例分割（OVIS）和开放集全景分割（OSPS）的挑战，这两项任务旨在无需详尽人工标注的情况下识别预定义和未见过的目标类别。现有方法通常存在伪掩码噪声大、视觉-文本关联不足，以及难以处理同义词或词表外（OOV）单词等问题。为克服这些挑战，我们提出了一个多模态框架，利用预训练的视觉-语言模型进行自动伪标签生成、基于CLIP的同义词过滤和基于GPT的caption重建。在我们的目标词汇辅助伪标签设置中，该框架首先利用Grounded SAM、LLaVA和CLIP构建伪分割掩码、描述性caption和语义对齐的同义词集，在无需人工标注的情况下提供多模态监督。随后，我们通过三个互补的训练目标来增强视觉-文本对齐：一个纳入视觉grounded同义词的扩展grounding损失、一个语义一致性损失，以及一个生成式caption重建损失。在COCO数据集上的大量实验表明，在该协议下，所提出方法持续优于先前最先进的方法，在OVIS和OSPS基准上均取得了显著改进。

</details>


### [35] [EGM-Det: Entropy-Guided Multimodal Adaptive Fusion for UAV RGB-IR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11685)
*Cunzheng Fan,Dawei Yan,Guanlin Wang,Xingshuo Yang,Yupeng Jia,Jing Yang,Haokui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EGM-Det，一个基于熵引导的多模态自适应融合框架，用于无人机视角下的RGB和红外目标检测，通过熵先验引导局部偏移对齐和空间-通道门控融合，并引入跨模态蒸馏来正则化融合门，在DroneVehicle、LLVIP和VEDAI三个基准上达到最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有大多数RGB-IR多模态融合方法使用静态或固定权重来融合特征，忽略了模态可靠性在空间上的变化，导致性能受限。

Method: 采用双流架构保留模态特定表示；提出熵偏移门控融合模块（Entropy Offset Gate Fusion），从输入强度、局部熵和跨模态差异中推导浅层熵先验，用于指导局部偏移对齐和空间-通道门控融合；引入跨模态蒸馏，每个学生分支从主分支匹配的跨模态教师分支中提取互补知识，并用熵自适应监督强调不确定的模态决策。

Result: 在DroneVehicle、LLVIP和VEDAI三个基准上均达到最先进性能，特别是在VEDAI上比之前方法提升超过10个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过熵引导的自适应融合和跨模态蒸馏正则化，EGM-Det能够有效选择性地聚合可靠的RGB和红外信息，显著提升无人机视角下多模态目标检测的性能。

Original Abstract: Joint use of RGB and infrared (IR) imagery can improve UAV-view object detection, but most existing methods fuse multimodal features with static or fixed weights and therefore overlook spatially varying modality reliability. We propose EGM-Det, an entropy-guided multimodal adaptive fusion framework for RGB-IR object detection. EGM-Det employs a dual-stream architecture to preserve modality-specific representations and introduces an Entropy Offset Gate Fusion module for adaptive multi-scale fusion. The module derives shallow entropy priors from input intensity, local entropy, and cross-modal discrepancy, and uses them to guide local offset alignment and spatial-channel gated fusion. It therefore selectively aggregates reliable RGB and infrared cues instead of uniformly combining heterogeneous features. We further introduce cross-modal distillation to regularize the learned fusion gates and reduce fusion degradation. Each student branch extracts complementary knowledge from the cross-modality teacher branch matched to the main branch, while entropy-adaptive supervision emphasizes uncertain modality decisions. Experiments on DroneVehicle, LLVIP, and VEDAI demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across all three benchmarks; in particular, EGM-Det outperforms prior approaches by more than 10 percentage points on VEDAI.

中文摘要翻译: 融合RGB和红外（IR）图像可以提升无人机视角下的目标检测性能，但大多数现有方法使用静态或固定权重来融合多模态特征，因此忽略了模态可靠性在空间上的变化。我们提出EGM-Det，一个熵引导的多模态自适应融合框架，用于RGB-IR目标检测。EGM-Det采用双流架构以保留模态特有的表示，并引入熵偏移门控融合模块（Entropy Offset Gate Fusion）实现自适应多尺度融合。该模块从输入强度、局部熵和跨模态差异中推导浅层熵先验，并用其指导局部偏移对齐和空间-通道门控融合。因此，它能够选择性地聚合可靠的RGB和红外线索，而非均匀地融合异质特征。我们进一步引入跨模态蒸馏来正则化学习到的融合门，减少融合退化。每个学生分支从与主分支匹配的跨模态教师分支中提取互补知识，而熵自适应监督强调不确定的模态决策。在DroneVehicle、LLVIP和VEDAI上的实验表明，EGM-Det在三个基准测试上均达到最先进性能；特别是，EGM-Det在VEDAI上比以往方法提升了超过10个百分点。

</details>


### [36] [Boundary-Enhanced Segmentation of Pig Point Clouds in Commercial Housing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11697)
*Zhankang Xu,Fei Shi,Xiangyu Qi,Zhaoyang Wang,Mengxin Guo,Yikai Fan,Simon X. Yang,Qifeng Li,Weihong Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于边界特征分析的猪点云分割方法，采用Octree Transformer作为骨干网络，结合软距离边界伪标签监督和双向跨边界语义模块，有效缓解了真实猪舍环境中点云边界粘连和背景误分割问题，在分割精度、mIoU和边界划分上均显著优于现有SOTA方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 真实猪舍环境中，猪点云与背景结构紧密接触，导致目标边界模糊、局部粘连和背景误分割，降低了后续点云补全和体尺测量的精度。现有方法难以有效处理这一边界粘连问题，亟需一种专门针对边界特征分析的点云分割方法。

Method: 该方法以Octree Transformer为骨干网络，通过八叉树卷积、自注意力编码和多尺度特征融合，整合局部几何细节与全局语义上下文；同时生成软距离边界伪标签以提供连续的边界监督，并设计双向跨边界语义模块，实现边界特征与语义特征之间的显式交互。

Result: 在综合数据集上的实验表明，该方法在分割精度、平均交并比（mIoU）和边界划分方面均显著优于多种先进模型，有效缓解了边界粘连问题。

Conclusion: 该方法的实验结果表明，基于边界特征分析的点云分割策略能够有效缓解边界粘连，为下游精细化畜牧养殖任务（如点云补全和体尺测量）提供可靠的点云输入。

Original Abstract: In real pigsty environments, pig point clouds often come into close contact with background structures, resulting in blurred target boundaries, local adhesion, and background mis-segmentation. This reduces the accuracy of subsequent point cloud completion and body size measurement. To address these challenges, this study proposes a pig point cloud segmentation method based on boundary feature analysis. The proposed method adopts Octree Transformer as the backbone network and integrates local geometric details with global semantic context through octree convolution, self-attention encoding, and multi-scale feature fusion. Furthermore, soft-distance boundary pseudo-labels are generated to provide continuous boundary supervision, and a bidirectional cross-boundary semantic module is designed to enable explicit interaction between boundary and semantic features. Experiments conducted on a comprehensive dataset demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms various state-of-the-art models in terms of segmentation accuracy, mean intersection over union, and boundary delineation. The results indicate that the method effectively alleviates boundary adhesion, providing reliable point cloud inputs for downstream precision livestock farming tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 在真实猪舍环境中，猪点云常与背景结构紧密接触，导致目标边界模糊、局部粘连和背景误分割，降低了后续点云补全和体尺测量的精度。为应对这些挑战，本研究提出了一种基于边界特征分析的猪点云分割方法。该方法采用Octree Transformer作为骨干网络，通过八叉树卷积、自注意力编码和多尺度特征融合，整合局部几何细节与全局语义上下文。此外，生成软距离边界伪标签以提供连续的边界监督，并设计双向跨边界语义模块，实现边界特征与语义特征的显式交互。在综合数据集上进行的实验表明，该方法在分割精度、平均交并比和边界划分方面均显著优于多种最先进模型。结果表明，该方法有效缓解了边界粘连，为下游精细化畜牧养殖任务提供了可靠的点云输入。

</details>


### [37] [STAR: A Spatial-Topology Aware Routing Framework for Generalizable 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11699)
*Mingwei Xing,Xinliang Wang,Yifeng Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STAR（空间拓扑感知路由框架），通过多属性自监督预训练分支和领域感知专家分支（包含DSR和EDA两种机制）解决跨模态3D场景理解中特征级MoE路由器未能充分表征局部采样拓扑的问题，在ScanNet和S3DIS等室内外场景任务上取得显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的仅基于特征的MoE路由器在语义监督下难以充分表征局部采样拓扑结构，导致在语义一致性与几何异质性并存时专家分配困难，限制了跨传感器模态统一3D场景理解模型的构建。

Method: STAR框架包含两个核心分支：(1) 多属性自监督预训练分支，覆盖拓扑和纹理变化，锚定跨域结构先验；(2) 领域感知专家分支，包含Domain-Spatial-Guided Routing (DSR)机制从空间上下文捕获局部拓扑变化，以及Entropy-controlled Dynamic Allocation (EDA)机制根据路由不确定性动态调整激活专家数量。两分支结合实现稳定的跨域表示学习与自适应专家分配。

Result: 在多个室内外场景任务上进行广泛实验，STAR在ScanNet验证集上达到80.1% mIoU，在S3DIS上达到77.2% mIoU，相比强基线持续提升。

Conclusion: STAR通过将空间拓扑感知的路由机制与自监督预训练相结合，有效解决了多域3D理解中专家分配困难的问题，验证了拓扑感知路由在跨模态3D场景理解中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Constructing a unified 3D scene understanding model has long been hindered by the topological discrepancies across sensor modalities. While applying the Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is a flexible approach for multi-domain 3D understanding, we observe that conventional feature-only MoE routers may underrepresent local sampling topology under semantic supervision, making expert allocation difficult when semantic consistency coexists with geometric heterogeneity. To overcome this challenge, we propose STAR (Spatial-Topology Aware Routing Framework). Specifically, we introduce a multi-attribute self-supervised pre-training branch, covering topological and textural variations, to anchor cross-domain structural priors. Building upon this, we design a domain-aware expert branch with two mechanisms: Domain-Spatial-Guided Routing (DSR), which captures local topological variations from spatial context, and Entropy-controlled Dynamic Allocation (EDA), which adjusts the number of activated experts according to routing uncertainty. Together, these branches combine stable cross-domain representation learning with adaptive expert allocation. Extensive experiments across various tasks, encompassing both indoor and outdoor scenes, demonstrate the effectiveness of STAR. It achieves 80.1% mIoU on the ScanNet validation set and 77.2% mIoU on S3DIS, consistently improving over strong baselines. Code is available at our project page (https://xmw666.github.io/STAR/).

中文摘要翻译: 构建统一的3D场景理解模型长期受到传感器模态间拓扑差异的阻碍。尽管采用混合专家（MoE）架构是多领域3D理解的一种灵活方案，但我们观察到，传统的仅基于特征的MoE路由器在语义监督下可能无法充分表征局部采样拓扑，导致在语义一致性与几何异质性并存时专家分配困难。为克服这一挑战，我们提出STAR（空间拓扑感知路由框架）。具体而言，我们引入一个多属性自监督预训练分支，覆盖拓扑与纹理变化，以锚定跨域结构先验。在此基础上，我们设计了一个领域感知专家分支，包含两种机制：域空间引导路由（DSR），从空间上下文中捕获局部拓扑变化；以及熵控动态分配（EDA），根据路由不确定性调整激活专家的数量。这些分支共同将稳定的跨域表示学习与自适应专家分配相结合。涵盖室内与室外场景的多种任务的广泛实验证明了STAR的有效性。它在ScanNet验证集上达到80.1% mIoU，在S3DIS上达到77.2% mIoU，相较于强基线持续提升。代码可在我们的项目页面获取（https://xmw666.github.io/STAR/）。

</details>


### [38] [Advancing MLLM-based UAV Image Understanding and Reasoning: A Benchmark and a Training-Free Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11738)
*Haoyu Zhang,Shuoxun Zhang,Peng Ye,Lin Zhang,Jiakang Yuan,Shenghong Yi,Yuening Wang,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLM在无人机航拍图像理解和推理方面的评估分散在各自独立的数据集和狭窄任务上，缺乏统一的评估基准，无法全面衡量模型在极端尺度变化、任意相机朝向和高目标密度等独特挑战下的能力。

Method: 首先构建UAVQA-Bench基准（1500个人工标注QA对，覆盖6个能力维度、16个任务，包含多项选择和视觉定位两种格式），系统评估多种开源和闭源MLLM及基于智能体的系统，识别出领域-工具集不匹配、未受控的错误传播和静态推理三种失败模式。据此提出UAV-MAS多智能体系统，包含三个模块：DSPE将查询路由至任务合适的视觉工具，CAIR验证中间推理以遏制错误累积，DAAS根据问题难度调整搜索深度。

Result: UAV-MAS在32B开源MLLM上达到77.0%总体准确率，超越Gemini 3 Pro 4.0%；8B变体在基线上提升8.7%。

Conclusion: 通过统一基准揭示MLLM在无人机航拍理解中的关键失败模式，并提出无需训练的UAV-MAS多智能体系统有效提升性能，且在不同模型规模上均展示出显著增益，验证了该方法在无人机航拍图像理解和推理任务中的通用性和有效性。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-based UAV aerial image understanding and reasoning is essential for aerial intelligence yet poses distinct challenges arising from extreme scale variation, arbitrary camera orientations, and high object density. Despite growing interest, existing evaluations remain fragmented across individual datasets and narrow tasks, leaving a critical gap in unified assessment of UAV understanding and reasoning capabilities. To fill this gap, we construct UAVQA-Bench, a benchmark of 1,500 human-annotated QA pairs drawn from 13 public UAV datasets, covering 6 capability dimensions and 16 tasks in both multiple-choice and visual grounding formats. Systematic evaluation of a broad range of open-source and closed-source MLLMs as well as agent-based systems on UAVQA-Bench identifies three key failure modes: domain-toolset mismatch, unchecked error propagation, and static reasoning. Motivated by these findings, we propose UAV-MAS, a training-free multi-agent system for MLLM-based UAV aerial image understanding and reasoning, comprising a Domain-Specific Perception Engine (DSPE) that routes queries to task-appropriate visual tools, a Context-Aware Iterative Refinement module (CAIR) that validates intermediate reasoning to curb error accumulation, and a Difficulty-Aware Adaptive Search mechanism (DAAS) that adjusts search depth to question difficulty. UAV-MAS with a 32B open-source MLLM achieves 77.0% overall accuracy on UAVQA-Bench, surpassing Gemini 3 Pro by 4.0\%, while the 8B variant improves 8.7\% over its base model.

中文摘要翻译: 基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的无人机航拍图像理解与推理对空中智能至关重要，但面临极端尺度变化、任意相机朝向和高目标密度等独特挑战。尽管关注度日益增长，现有评估仍分散在各自独立的数据集和狭窄任务上，在无人机理解与推理能力的统一评估方面存在关键缺口。为填补这一空白，我们构建了UAVQA-Bench，一个包含从13个公开UAV数据集中提取的1500个人工标注QA对的基准，覆盖6个能力维度和16个任务，采用多项选择和视觉定位两种格式。对UAVQA-Bench上广泛的开放和闭源MLLM以及基于智能体的系统进行系统评估，识别出三种关键失败模式：领域-工具集不匹配、未受控的错误传播和静态推理。基于这些发现，我们提出UAV-MAS，一种无需训练的面向MLLM无人机航拍图像理解与推理的多智能体系统，包含将查询路由至任务合适的视觉工具的领域特定感知引擎（DSPE）、验证中间推理以遏制错误累积的上下文感知迭代精化模块（CAIR），以及根据问题难度调整搜索深度的难度感知自适应搜索机制（DAAS）。搭载32B开源MLLM的UAV-MAS在UAVQA-Bench上达到77.0%的总体准确率，超越Gemini 3 Pro 4.0%，而8B变体相比其基础模型提升8.7%。

### [39] [JieZi: A Large-Scale Expert-Audited Dataset and Benchmark for Ancient Chinese Character Exegesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11741)
*Ran Li,Huiguo He,Jiahuan Cao,Junle Liu,Hiuyi Cheng,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出古汉字训诂（ACCE）这一视觉-语言问答任务，模拟学者对古汉字的训诂过程，并构建了大规模专家审核的训练数据集JieZi-Dataset（50万+QA对）和人工专家构建的评测基准JieZi-Bench。实验表明当前多模态大模型在基础识别上表现良好，但在字形分析、语义推理和历时演变理解上存在困难，微调后可全面提升各层级性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有计算手段仅聚焦于古汉字识别和检索等子任务，缺乏支持全面学术训诂分析所需的结构化数据集和基准，无法满足将视觉观察、语言分析与历史背景相结合的综合训诂需求。

Method: 提出ACCE任务，将其组织为四个递进层级（基础字符识别、字形分析、字义训诂、历时演变分析）。构建JieZi-Dataset，采用专家设计的模板和源文本引用约束生成流程以减少事实错误，并在关键阶段进行人工审核；构建由人类专家编制和验证的JieZi-Bench评测基准，其参考答案源自与训练数据分离的权威词典著作。

Result: 实验表明：当前多模态大语言模型在基础识别层级表现良好，但在字形分析、语义推理和历时演变理解上表现不佳；在JieZi-Dataset上微调后，模型在四个层级的性能均得到显著提升。

Conclusion: 所构建的数据集和基准有效支撑了古汉字训诂的VQA研究，且微调能够实质性地增强多模态模型在古汉字训诂各层级任务上的能力，为后续研究提供了开源资源。

Original Abstract: The scholarly exegesis of ancient Chinese characters demands integrating visual observation, linguistic analysis, and historical context. However, existing computational approaches focus narrowly on subtasks such as character recognition and retrieval, lacking the structured datasets and benchmarks required for comprehensive scholarly analysis. To address this limitation, we introduce Ancient Chinese Character Exegesis (ACCE), a vision-language question answering (VQA) task that models the scholarly exegesis process. ACCE is organized into four progressive levels: basic character identification, glyph-form analysis, meaning exegesis, and diachronic evolution analysis. To support this task, we construct two complementary resources. JieZi-Dataset is the first large-scale, expert-audited VQA training dataset for ACCE, comprising over 500K QA pairs. It is constructed via a pipeline that reduces factual errors by constraining generation with expert-designed templates and source-text references. Human verification is further applied at each key stage to ensure scholarly accuracy. JieZi-Bench is an evaluation benchmark aligned with the exegesis process, constructed and verified by human experts to ensure evaluation reliability. It consists of four levels with reference answers curated from authoritative lexicographic works held separate from the training data. Experiments on multimodal large language models show that current models perform well on basic identification but struggle with glyph analysis, semantic reasoning, and diachronic understanding. Fine-tuning on JieZi-Dataset substantially improves performance across all four levels. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/Ran00w/JieZi.

中文摘要翻译: 古汉字的学术训诂需要整合视觉观察、语言学分析与历史背景。然而，现有的计算方法仅窄泛地关注字符识别和检索等子任务，缺乏支持全面学术分析所需的结构化数据集和基准。为解决这一局限，我们提出古汉字训诂（ACCE）——一个模拟学术训诂过程的视觉-语言问答（VQA）任务。ACCE被组织为四个递进层级：基础字符识别、字形分析、字义训诂和历时演变分析。为支持该任务，我们构建了两类互补资源。JieZi-Dataset是首个面向ACCE的大规模、专家审核的VQA训练数据集，包含超过50万对问答对。其构建流程通过使用专家设计的模板和源文本引用约束生成过程，有效减少事实性错误；在每个关键阶段进一步应用人工验证以确保学术准确性。JieZi-Bench是一个与训诂过程对齐的评测基准，由人类专家构建和验证以保证评测可靠性。它包含四个层级，其参考答案取自与训练数据分离的权威词典著作。在多模态大语言模型上的实验表明，当前模型在基础识别上表现良好，但在字形分析、语义推理和历时演变理解上存在困难。在JieZi-Dataset上进行微调可显著提升四个层级的性能。代码和数据集可在https://github.com/Ran00w/JieZi获取。

</details>


### [40] [LiveAnimate: Stable Long-Form Streaming Human Animation in Real-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11745)
*Yuxuan Zhang,Haozhong Xiong,Yubo Huang,Jiayi Song,Jinpeng Yu,Haofan Wang,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang,Liwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiveAnimate是首个将实时流式生成与长时稳定生成结合在十亿参数规模上的角色动画系统，基于14B参数的视频扩散Transformer（DiT），通过两阶段训练（参考锚定教师强制自适应+块级自强制蒸馏）将采样预算降至3步，并引入Pose-Retrieval Sink Attention（PR-Sink）有界KV缓存机制，在两张H100上实现19.63 FPS的流式推理，三分钟基准测试中保持近乎恒定的感知质量和身份一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实时交互应用（如直播、远程呈现、虚拟化身）需要实时生成角色动画，但现有基于扩散的系统每段视频需要数分钟到数小时，无法满足实时交互需求。现有方法在长序列生成中还会出现质量退化和身份不一致的问题。

Method: 基于14B参数的扩散视频Transformer（DiT）构建LiveAnimate。采用两阶段训练：首先通过Reference-Anchored Teacher-Forcing Adaptation将预训练的双向DiT适配为块级因果自回归生成器；然后通过Block-wise Self-Forcing Distillation将采样预算减少到3步。引入PR-Sink有界KV缓存机制，包含永久锚定首个生成块的Static Sink、保存姿态检索历史块的Dynamic Sink以及三槽滚动窗口，在姿态重复时恢复相关外观上下文而无需保留整个序列，使内存和逐块延迟保持恒定。结合Ulysses序列并行和算子融合，在两张NVIDIA H100上实现19.63 FPS流式推理。

Result: 在两张NVIDIA H100 GPU上实现19.63 FPS的流式推理。在三分钟基准测试中，从最初30秒到最后1分钟保持近乎恒定的感知质量和身份一致性（IQA 4.047 vs 4.026），而现有系统在相同场景下严重退化或需要数小时离线计算。

Conclusion: LiveAnimate在质量、延迟和时长三个维度上为交互式全身动画建立了新的操作点，证明了扩散模型在实时长视频生成中的可行性，同时解决了长流中外观保持和计算效率的核心挑战。

Original Abstract: Pose-driven human animation synthesizes a video of a target person from a single reference image and a driving pose stream. Real-time generation is essential for interactive applications such as live streaming, telepresence, and virtual avatars, yet diffusion-based systems require minutes to hours per clip, precluding responsive interaction. We present LiveAnimate, to our knowledge the first animation system to combine real-time streaming with stable long-form generation at billion scale, built on a 14B-parameter video Diffusion Transformer (DiT). A two-stage training pipeline first adapts a pretrained bidirectional DiT into a block-causal autoregressive generator through Reference-Anchored Teacher-Forcing Adaptation, and then reduces the sampling budget to three steps through Block-wise Self-Forcing Distillation. To preserve appearance over extended streams, we introduce Pose-Retrieval Sink Attention (PR-Sink), a bounded KV-cache mechanism combining a Static Sink that permanently anchors the first generated block, a Dynamic Sink that holds a pose-retrieved historical block, and a three-slot Rolling Window. When a pose recurs, PR-Sink restores the relevant appearance context without retaining the entire sequence, so memory and per-block latency remain constant regardless of stream duration. Together with Ulysses sequence parallelism and operator fusion, these designs enable 19.63\,FPS streaming inference on two NVIDIA H100 GPUs. On a three-minute benchmark, LiveAnimate maintains nearly constant perceptual quality and identity from the first 30 seconds to the final minute (IQA 4.047 vs.\ 4.026), while prior systems degrade substantially or require hours of offline computation for the same rollout. These results establish a new operating point in quality, latency, and duration for interactive full-body animation.

中文摘要翻译: 姿态驱动的人类动画从单张参考图像和驱动的姿态流合成目标人物的视频。实时生成对于直播、远程呈现和虚拟化身等交互应用至关重要，然而基于扩散的系统生成每段视频需要数分钟至数小时，无法实现响应式交互。我们提出LiveAnimate，据我们所知这是首个在十亿参数规模上结合实时流式生成与稳定长时生成的动画系统，构建于14B参数的视频扩散Transformer（DiT）之上。两阶段训练流程首先通过参考锚定教师强制自适应（Reference-Anchored Teacher-Forcing Adaptation）将预训练的双向DiT适配为块级因果自回归生成器，随后通过块级自强制蒸馏（Block-wise Self-Forcing Distillation）将采样预算减少至三步。为在扩展流中保持外观，我们引入姿态检索汇聚注意力（Pose-Retrieval Sink Attention, PR-Sink），这是一种有界KV缓存机制，结合了永久锚定首个生成块的静态汇聚（Static Sink）、保存姿态检索历史块的动态汇聚（Dynamic Sink）以及三槽滚动窗口。当姿态重复出现时，PR-Sink在无需保留整个序列的情况下恢复相关外观上下文，因此内存和逐块延迟随流时长保持恒定。结合Ulysses序列并行与算子融合，这些设计在两张NVIDIA H100 GPU上实现了19.63 FPS的流式推理。在三分钟基准测试中，LiveAnimate从最初30秒到最后1分钟保持近乎恒定的感知质量和身份一致性（IQA 4.047对4.026），而现有系统在相同滚动生成中严重退化或需要数小时离线计算。这些结果为交互式全身动画在质量、延迟和时长方面确立了新的操作点。

</details>


### [41] [Making Every Step Count: Spatio-Temporal Information Allocation for Imaging Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11747)
*Yi Cao,Xiangyong Cao,Pei Liu,Yong-Jin Liu,Deyu Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两个即插即用且无需训练的组件——频谱自适应调度（SAS）和测量优先注意力（MPA），分别解决基于流的生成模型在无训练逆问题求解中的时间分配和空间信息传播问题，可显著提升现有流式逆求解器的图像恢复质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于流的逆问题求解器主要关注单步更新的设计，忽视了在固定函数评估次数（NFEs）下的时空信息分配问题。时间上，早期探索不足会使流轨迹陷入错误的语义盆地，而早期分配过多NFEs则留给后期细化阶段的预算不足；空间上，数据一致性仅在观测区域提供直接约束，缺失区域的恢复主要依赖生成先验。这两个问题尚未被系统解决。

Method: 提出两个互补且无需训练的组件：（1）频谱自适应调度（Spectrum-Adaptive Scheduling, SAS），根据退化频谱和logSNR几何特性在流时间上分配可用NFEs，以更好地平衡语义探索与细节细化；（2）测量优先注意力（Measurement-Prioritized Attention, MPA），利用数据与先验之间的冲突，引导信息流向约束较弱的区域，增强语义和结构保真度。

Result: 在超分辨率、运动去模糊和图像修复等标准图像逆问题上进行了大量实验，结果表明所提组件能够以即插即用的方式集成到现有基于流的逆求解器中，无需重新训练或额外的流模型评估，且能显著提升现有求解器的恢复质量。

Conclusion: 通过SAS和MPA分别解决时空两个维度的信息分配问题，可以有效增强基于流的生成模型在无训练逆问题求解中的性能，为现有求解器提供了一种轻量级、通用且有效的增强方案。

Original Abstract: Flow-based generative models have emerged as powerful image priors for training-free inverse problem solving, capturing coherent semantics and fine-grained structure. Despite these strengths, existing flow-based inverse solvers primarily focus on the design of individual updates, largely overlooking spatio-temporal information allocation under a fixed number of function evaluations (NFEs). Temporally, insufficient early exploration can trap the flow trajectory in an incorrect semantic basin, whereas excessive allocation of NFEs to early stages leaves little budget for late-stage refinement. Spatially, data consistency provides direct constraints only within observed regions, whereas the recovery of missing regions relies mainly on the generative prior. To address these two issues, we introduce two complementary and training-free components, i.e., Spectrum-Adaptive Scheduling (SAS) and Measurement-Prioritized Attention (MPA). For temporal allocation, SAS distributes the available NFEs over flow time according to the degradation spectrum and logSNR geometry, thus better balancing semantic exploration and detail refinement. For spatial propagation, MPA exploits data-prior conflicts to guide information toward weakly constrained regions, thereby enhancing semantic and structural fidelity. Extensive experiments on standard image inverse problems, e.g., super-resolution, motion deblurring, and inpainting, demonstrate that the proposed components can be integrated into existing flow-based inverse solvers in a plug-and-play manner without retraining or additional flow-model evaluations, and can also significantly improve the restoration quality of existing solvers.

中文摘要翻译: 基于流的生成模型因其能够捕捉连贯语义和精细结构，已成为无训练逆问题求解中强大的图像先验。尽管具有这些优势，现有的基于流的逆求解器主要关注单步更新的设计，在很大程度上忽略了固定函数评估次数（NFEs）下时空信息分配的问题。在时间维度上，早期探索不足会使流轨迹陷入错误的语义盆地，而将过多NFEs分配给早期阶段又会使后期细化阶段预算不足。在空间维度上，数据一致性仅能在观测区域内提供直接约束，而缺失区域的恢复主要依赖于生成先验。为了解决这两个问题，我们引入了两个互补且无需训练的组件，即频谱自适应调度（SAS）和测量优先注意力（MPA）。在时间分配方面，SAS根据退化频谱和logSNR几何特性在流时间上分配可用的NFEs，从而更好地平衡语义探索与细节细化。在空间传播方面，MPA利用数据-先验冲突来引导信息流向约束较弱的区域，从而增强语义和结构保真度。在标准图像逆问题（如超分辨率、运动去模糊和图像修复）上的大量实验表明，所提出的组件能够以即插即用的方式集成到现有基于流的逆求解器中，无需重新训练或额外的流模型评估，并且能够显著提升现有求解器的恢复质量。

</details>


### [42] [Dual Modality Prompted Diffusion Priors for Zero Shot Hyperspectral Pansharpening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11748)
*Pengwei Xie,Fei Zhu,Jiajun Li,Xiangyuan Liu,Xiangyuan Liu,Kangqing Shen,Gemine Vivone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于零样本高光谱全色锐化（pansharpening）的双模态图像提示扩散模型（DIDM），通过将低分辨率高光谱和全色影像编码为谱域与空间域提示令牌并注入冻结的遥感扩散模型，实现空间细节与光谱保真的平衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散的方法利用预训练图像先验生成低维表示再映射到高光谱域，但观测到的全色和高光谱图像仅通过外部重建目标施加约束，限制了它们与扩散先验的直接交互，导致先验信息与观测数据之间缺乏有效融合。

Method: DIDM将LRHS和PAN观测分别编码为光谱提示令牌和空间提示令牌，通过交叉注意力机制注入到冻结的遥感扩散模型中间特征中，使互补的光谱和空间信息直接引导扩散特征演化；同时引入全色引导的加权像素感知总变差正则化器，结合低分辨率高光谱退化保真度、全色响应保真度和梯度自适应结构正则化，在保留结构不连续性的同时抑制均匀区域的伪变化。

Result: 在Pavia、Chikusei和Houston数据集上的降分辨率协议实验中，DIDM在所有评估指标上均取得最佳性能；在FR1数据集上的全分辨率评估中获得了最高的HQNR指标。

Conclusion: 内部双模态提示和全色引导的结构正则化能够在空间细节增强与光谱保持之间提供有效的平衡，验证了DIDM方法的有效性和优越性。

Original Abstract: Hyperspectral pansharpening aims to reconstruct a high resolution hyperspectral (HRHS) image from a panchromatic (PAN) image and a low resolution hyperspectral (LRHS) image while preserving both spatial details and spectral fidelity. Recent diffusion based methods exploit pretrained image priors by generating a low dimensional representation and subsequently mapping it to the hyperspectral domain. However, the observed panchromatic and hyperspectral images are typically imposed only through external reconstruction objectives, limiting their direct interaction with the diffusion prior. To address this issue, we propose dual-modality image-prompted diffusion model (DIDM) for zero shot hyperspectral pansharpening. DIDM encodes the low resolution hyperspectral and panchromatic observations into spectral and spatial prompt tokens, respectively, and injects them into intermediate features of a frozen remote sensing diffusion model through cross attention, allowing complementary spectral and spatial information to directly guide diffusion feature evolution. In addition, we introduce a panchromatic guided weighted pixel aware total variation regularizer that combines low resolution hyperspectral degradation fidelity and panchromatic response fidelity with gradient adaptive structural regularization, thereby preserving structural discontinuities while suppressing spurious variations in homogeneous regions. Extensive experiments on Pavia, Chikusei, and Houston under reduced resolution protocols show that DIDM achieves the best performance across all evaluated metrics, while full resolution evaluation on FR1 yields the highest HQNR among the compared methods. These results demonstrate that internal dual modality prompting and panchromatic guided structural regularization provide an effective balance between spatial detail enhancement and spectral preservation.

中文摘要翻译: 高光谱全色锐化旨在从一幅全色（PAN）图像和一幅低分辨率高光谱（LRHS）图像重建高分辨率高光谱（HRHS）图像，同时保持空间细节和光谱保真度。现有的基于扩散的方法通过生成低维表示并随后将其映射到高光谱域来利用预训练图像先验。然而，观测到的全色和高光谱图像通常仅通过外部重建目标施加约束，限制了它们与扩散先验的直接交互。为解决这一问题，我们提出了双模态图像提示扩散模型（DIDM），用于零样本高光谱全色锐化。DIDM分别将低分辨率高光谱和全色观测编码为光谱提示令牌和空间提示令牌，并通过交叉注意力将其注入到冻结的遥感扩散模型的中间特征中，使得互补的光谱和空间信息能够直接引导扩散特征的演化。此外，我们引入了一种全色引导的加权像素感知总变差正则化器，将低分辨率高光谱退化保真度和全色响应保真度与梯度自适应结构正则化相结合，从而在抑制均匀区域伪变化的同时保持结构不连续性。在Pavia、Chikusei和Houston数据集上的降分辨率协议下进行的大量实验表明，DIDM在所有评估指标上均取得了最佳性能，而在FR1数据集上的全分辨率评估中，DIDM在比较方法中获得了最高的HQNR。这些结果表明，内部双模态提示和全色引导的结构正则化能够在空间细节增强与光谱保持之间提供有效的平衡。

</details>


### [43] [UniSwap: Streaming Audio-Visual Identity Swapping for Talking Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11752)
*Yuxuan Zhang,Haozhong Xiong,Jiayi Song,Jinpeng Yu,Yang Shi,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang,Liwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniSwap是首个在对话视频中实现流式联合音视频身份替换的框架，通过单一音频-视觉扩散Transformer同时迁移参考人物的外观与音色，并采用swap-and-reconstruct管线、条件流式自适应、高效自强制DMD及多LoRA切换等技术，实现了音视频同步、身份保持和长时稳定生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有说话视频中的人物替换方法对视觉和语音两种模态分别使用独立优化的模型，难以保证音视频的一致性。此外，跨身份对齐训练数据稀缺，且缺乏一个统一的框架来同时协调外观和声音的迁移，同时保持源视频的运动、场景、语言内容和音视频时序。

Method: UniSwap采用单一音频-视觉扩散Transformer实现外观和音色的联合替换。具体包括：(1) swap-and-reconstruct管线，从真实片段中移除视觉和语音身份信息并以原始片段作为重建目标，解决跨身份训练对稀缺问题；(2) In-context Pretraining实现联合替换；(3) Conditional Streaming Adaptation实现块因果KV缓存生成；(4) Efficient Self-forcing DMD缓解暴露偏差并将去噪步骤从30步降至3步；(5) Efficient Multi-LoRA Switching使三种DMD角色共享单一冻结骨干网络；(6) Feature-RoPE Decomposition保持缓存位置在训练范围内，支持稳定的长序列推理。

Result: 实验表明UniSwap在音视频同步、身份保持、流式推理效率以及长序列生成稳定性方面均表现出色，同时将采样步数从30步显著减少到每块3步，支持高效的流式生成。

Conclusion: UniSwap通过统一的音频-视觉扩散Transformer框架成功实现了说话视频中的流式联合音视频身份替换，有效解决了多模态一致性、训练数据稀缺、暴露偏差和长序列推理稳定性等关键问题，为该领域提供了一个高效且统一的基础框架。

Original Abstract: Talking-video character replacement requires coordinated transfer of appearance and voice while preserving the source motion, scene, linguistic content, and audio-video timing. Existing methods use separately optimized models for the two modalities, making audio-visual consistency difficult to enforce. We present UniSwap, the first framework for streaming joint audio-visual identity replacement in talking videos. Given a source video, a reference image, and a reference voice clip, UniSwap transfers the reference appearance and vocal timbre within a single audio-visual diffusion transformer while preserving the source content and dynamics. To address the scarcity of aligned cross-identity training pairs, we introduce a swap-and-reconstruct pipeline that removes visual and vocal identity from real clips and uses the original clips as reconstruction targets. Starting from a bidirectional backbone, we progressively adapt the model through In-context Pretraining for joint replacement, Conditional Streaming Adaptation for block-causal KV-cached generation, and Efficient Self-forcing DMD for mitigating exposure bias and reducing sampling from 30 to 3 denoising steps per block. Efficient Multi-LoRA Switching enables the three DMD roles to share a single frozen backbone. Feature-RoPE Decomposition keeps cached positions within the training range, supporting stable long-form inference. Experiments demonstrate strong audio-visual synchronization, competitive identity preservation, efficient streaming, and stable long-form generation.

中文摘要翻译: 说话视频中的人物替换需要在外观和声音的协调迁移之间取得平衡，同时保持源视频的运动、场景、语言内容和音视频时序。现有方法对两种模态分别使用独立优化的模型，导致音视频一致性难以保证。我们提出UniSwap，这是首个用于说话视频中流式联合音视频身份替换的框架。给定源视频、参考图像和参考语音片段，UniSwap在单一的音频-视觉扩散Transformer内迁移参考外观和声音音色，同时保留源内容与动态。为解决对齐的跨身份训练对稀缺的问题，我们引入了一种swap-and-reconstruct管线，从真实片段中移除视觉和语音身份，并以原始片段作为重建目标。从双向骨干网络出发，我们通过以下步骤逐步适配模型：用于联合替换的In-context预训练、用于块因果KV缓存生成的条件流式适配，以及用于缓解暴露偏差并将每块去噪步骤从30步减少到3步的高效自强制DMD。高效多LoRA切换使三种DMD角色共享一个冻结的骨干网络。特征RoPE分解将缓存位置保持在训练范围内，支持稳定的长序列推理。实验证明了强音视频同步、有竞争力的身份保持、高效的流式生成以及稳定的长序列生成能力。

</details>


### [44] [Automated binary classification of hazelnut X-ray images: A deep-learning benchmark for quality assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11759)
*Giancarlo Sportelli,Nicola Belcari,Roberta Pace,Umberto Bernardo,Sharmin Sultana,Alessandra Toncelli,Matteo Giaccone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于X射线成像的榛子质量二元分类基准（健康vs.缺陷），包含799张单仁分割图像和101个采集单元。通过群组式划分交叉验证评估了7种单模型配置和10种概率聚合集成方法，发现二元交叉熵训练的CNN与冻结Swin Transformer的平均概率集成表现最佳（平均平衡准确率86.3%±1.8%），并验证了专家重新标注和多样本划分评估对小型不平衡农业数据集的重要性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 非破坏性X射线成像能揭示榛子外部难以观察的内部缺陷，但由于类别间X射线影像差异细微、类别严重不平衡以及标注数据有限，自动化解读仍具挑战。因此需要一个系统化的基准来严格评估深度学习模型在该任务上的表现。

Method: 基于799张分割后的单仁X射线图像（224×224灰度）构建基准，分属101个采集单元。采用群组式划分（split-rotation）协议，使用5个不同随机种子生成的数据划分，在验证集上选择决策阈值，并在验证集和测试集上进行确定性评估。评估了7种单模型配置和10种概率聚合集成方法，并对比了专家重新标注前后的标注条件。

Result: 在专家重新标注条件下，二元交叉熵训练的CNN与冻结Swin Transformer的平均概率集成取得最高平均平衡准确率（86.3%±1.8%）。各方法间存在显著的划分间变异性，表明多划分评估对可靠比较至关重要。专家重新标注使全部17种方法的性能提升2.8-8.1个百分点，但对跨划分方差影响有限。

Conclusion: 深度学习在基于X射线的自动化榛子质量评估中具有潜力，但在小型、不平衡的农业成像数据集中，严格的评估协议和标签质量控制（如专家重新标注）对获得可靠、可比的结果至关重要。

Original Abstract: Non-destructive X-ray imaging can reveal internal hazelnut defects that are difficult to detect by external inspection alone; however, automated interpretation remains challenging because of subtle radiographic differences among classes, marked class imbalance, and limited annotated data. Here, we present a benchmark for binary hazelnut quality classification (healthy versus defective) based on 799 segmented single-kernel X-ray images (224 x 224 pixels, grayscale), grouped into 101 acquisition units. Seven single-model configurations and ten probability-aggregation ensembles were evaluated using a group-wise split-rotation protocol across five data splits generated using different random seeds. Decision thresholds were selected on the validation set, and performance was assessed deterministically on validation and test sets. Under the expert-reassessed annotation condition, the average-probability ensemble of the binary cross-entropy-trained convolutional neural network and frozen Swin Transformer achieved the highest mean balanced accuracy (86.3% +/- 1.8%, five seeds), with several other ensembles providing comparable performance. Across methods, substantial split-to-split variability was observed, indicating that multi-split evaluation is essential for reliable model comparison at this dataset scale. Expert reassessment of ambiguous samples improved the performance of all 17 evaluated methods by 2.8-8.1 percentage points, while having only a limited effect on cross-split variance. The results highlight both the potential of deep learning for automated X-ray-based hazelnut quality assessment and the importance of rigorous evaluation and label curation in small, imbalanced agricultural imaging datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 非破坏性X射线成像能够揭示仅靠外部检查难以发现的榛子内部缺陷；然而，由于类别间的射线影像差异细微、类别严重不平衡以及标注数据有限，自动化解读仍具挑战。本文提出了一个基于799张分割后的单仁X射线图像（224×224像素，灰度）的榛子质量二元分类基准（健康vs.缺陷），共分为101个采集单元。我们采用基于群组式划分的交叉验证协议，使用不同随机种子生成的五个数据划分，评估了七种单模型配置和十种概率聚合集成方法。决策阈值在验证集上选取，性能在验证集和测试集上以确定性方式评估。在专家重新评估标注的条件下，二元交叉熵训练的卷积神经网络与冻结Swin Transformer的平均概率集成取得了最高的平均平衡准确率（86.3%±1.8%，五个种子），其他若干集成方法也提供了可比性能。总体上，各方法间存在显著的划分间变异性，表明在该数据集规模下，多划分评估对于可靠的模型比较至关重要。对模糊样本进行专家重新评估使全部17种评估方法的性能提升了2.8至8.1个百分点，而对跨划分方差的影响有限。这些结果既凸显了深度学习在基于X射线的榛子质量自动评估方面的潜力，也强调了在小型、不平衡的农业成像数据集中进行严格评估和标注管理的重要性。

</details>


### [45] [ProBAG: Prototype-Guided Boundary-Aware Graph Diffusion for Weakly Supervised Histopathology Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11765)
*Duy-Dong Nguyen,Le-Van Thai,Hoai Nhan Pham,Ngoc Lam Quang Bui,Tam Tran,Zhi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProBAG是一种弱监督语义分割的第一阶段伪掩码生成器，结合数据集视觉原型与病理学对齐的CONCH文本原型，利用多尺度冻结UNI特征，通过类级功率重校准和一阶梯图扩散机制生成无需CRF的伪掩码，在BCSS-WSSS和LUAD-HistoSeg数据集上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 弱监督语义分割可利用图像级标注避免昂贵的像素级标注，但基于CAM的方法往往只定位高度判别性区域，且在组织界面附近不可靠。作者旨在解决CAM方法在组织学图像中判别区域定位不充分、边界不可靠的问题。

Method: ProBAG是一个阶段一伪掩码生成器，包含两大互补机制：(1) 类级功率重校准（class-wise power recalibration），重塑类间竞争同时保持每个像素的总前景激活质量；(2) 一步图扩散（one-step graph diffusion），利用后期Transformer注意力上下文差异作为软结构边界线索来惩罚特征亲和度。方法融合数据集特定视觉原型与病理学对齐的CONCH文本原型，基于多尺度冻结UNI特征。生成的伪掩码无需CRF或外部分割模型，并可用于监督下游Phikon-FPN分割器完成两阶段对比。

Result: 在BCSS-WSSS和LUAD-HistoSeg数据集上，ProBAG一致性地优于最近的WSSS方法。消融实验表明，病理学对齐的文本语义提供了最大的性能提升，而图细化提供了较小但互补的增益。

Conclusion: ProBAG通过多模态原型（视觉+病理文本）与图扩散细化相结合，有效提升了组织病理学弱监督语义分割的伪掩码质量，且无需CRF后处理或外部分割模型，验证了病理文本先验知识在弱监督分割任务中的关键价值。

Original Abstract: Weakly supervised semantic segmentation enables histopathology tissue segmentation from image-level annotations, avoiding costly pixel-level labeling by expert pathologists. However, CAM-based methods often localize only highly discriminative regions and remain unreliable near tissue interfaces. We propose ProBAG, a stage-1 pseudo-mask generator that combines dataset-specific visual prototypes with pathology-aligned CONCH text prototypes over multi-scale frozen UNI features. ProBAG introduces two complementary mechanisms: class-wise power recalibration that reshapes inter-class competition while preserving the total foreground activation mass at each pixel, and one-step graph diffusion in which feature affinities are penalized by a late-transformer attention-context discrepancy used as a soft structural boundary cue. The resulting stage-1 pseudo-masks require neither CRF nor an external segmentation model; for complete two-stage comparison, they additionally supervise a downstream Phikon-FPN segmenter. Experiments on BCSS-WSSS and LUAD-HistoSeg show consistent gains over recent WSSS approaches, while ablations indicate that pathology-aligned text semantics provide the largest improvement and graph refinement provides a smaller complementary gain. The code is available at: https://github.com/wterrr/WSSS

中文摘要翻译: 弱监督语义分割使得组织病理学组织分割仅需图像级标注即可实现，避免了病理专家昂贵的像素级标注。然而，基于CAM的方法往往只定位高度判别性区域，并且在组织界面附近不可靠。我们提出ProBAG，一个阶段一伪掩码生成器，它将数据集特定的视觉原型与病理学对齐的CONCH文本原型相结合，基于多尺度冻结的UNI特征。ProBAG引入了两种互补机制：类级功率重校准，它在重塑类间竞争的同时保持每个像素的总前景激活质量；以及一步图扩散，其中特征亲和度由后期Transformer注意力上下文差异作为软结构边界线索进行惩罚。所生成的阶段一伪掩码既不需要CRF，也不需要外部分割模型；为完成两阶段对比，它们额外监督一个下游的Phikon-FPN分割器。在BCSS-WSSS和LUAD-HistoSeg上的实验表明，ProBAG一致性地优于最近的WSSS方法，而消融实验表明病理学对齐的文本语义提供了最大的改进，图细化提供了较小但互补的增益。代码可在https://github.com/wterrr/WSSS获取。

</details>


### [46] [Achieving Near-Zero-Overhead Multi-Model Hierarchical Classification in Real-Time Detection Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11770)
*Vaishnav Raju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套五步方法论，实现在NVIDIA Jetson DLA神经加速器上零GPU回退的INT8分类骨干网络部署，并通过GPU检测器与DLA分类器的并发流水线，在Jetson Orin NX上实现了接近零的流水线开销（12.5 vs 13.3 FPS）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 边缘部署的视觉系统（目标识别、监控、自动驾驶、无人机等）需要层级推理流水线（检测模型+下游精细分类器）。将全部模型运行在GPU上会形成串行瓶颈，限制实时吞吐量。虽然现代边缘SoC将GPU与专用神经加速器（NPU/DLA）配对支持并发执行，但将自定义模型部署到这些加速器上因严格的算子约束、量化不兼容和缺乏文档化的端到端流程而难以实现。

Method: 提出五步方法论：(1) 架构适配；(2) 手动动态范围变通方案，挽救TensorRT隐式量化（将准确率从75%恢复到94.0%）以快速验证流水线；(3) 量化感知训练；(4) ONNX图手术以实现DLA编译；(5) 并发GPU检测/DLA分类推理流水线。同时记录了九个工程约束及根因分析和可泛化的解决方案。

Result: 在Jetson Orin NX上，双头人物属性分类器在DLA上运行与GPU目标检测器并发，实现了接近零的流水线开销（12.5 vs 13.3 FPS，检测器单独运行于1080p），双DLA扩展无额外成本。隐式量化的手动动态范围变通方案恢复了94.0%的准确率（相较于隐式量化的75%）。

Conclusion: 该方法是骨干网络无关的，可泛化到任何检测-分类边缘推理流水线，证明了在边缘SoC上利用专用神经加速器实现高效并发推理的可行性。

Original Abstract: Edge-deployed vision systems in target recognition, surveillance, autonomous vehicles, and drone domains require hierarchical inference pipelines where a detection model identifies objects of interest and downstream classifiers provide fine-grained attribute analysis. Running all models on the GPU creates a serial bottleneck that limits real-time throughput as pipeline stages grow. Modern edge SoCs pair GPUs with dedicated neural accelerators (NPUs, DLAs) capable of concurrent execution, yet deploying custom models on these accelerators remains impractical due to strict operator constraints, quantization incompatibilities, and an undocumented end-to-end pipeline. We target NVIDIA Jetson DLA cores as the representative platform. We present a five-step methodology for zero GPU fallback DLA INT8 deployment of classification backbones, comprising architecture adaptation, manual dynamic range workaround to rescue TensorRT's implicit quantization (recovering 94.0% accuracy from implicit quantization's 75%) for rapid pipeline validation before explicit quantization, quantization-aware training, ONNX graph surgery for DLA compilation, and a concurrent GPU-detection/DLA-classification inference pipeline. We document nine engineering constraints with root-cause analysis and generalizable solutions. Validation on a dual-head person attribute classifier running on DLA alongside a GPU object detector on a Jetson Orin NX demonstrates near-zero pipeline overhead (12.5 vs. 13.3~FPS detector-only at 1080p), with dual-DLA scaling at no additional cost. The methodology is backbone-agnostic and generalizes to any detection-classification edge pipeline.

中文摘要翻译: 在目标识别、监控、自动驾驶和无人机等领域，边缘视觉系统需要层级推理流水线，其中检测模型识别感兴趣目标，下游分类器提供细粒度属性分析。将所有模型运行在GPU上会形成串行瓶颈，随着流水线阶段增多而限制实时吞吐量。现代边缘SoC将GPU与专用神经加速器（NPU、DLA）配对支持并发执行，但由于严格的算子约束、量化不兼容以及缺乏有文档记载的端到端流水线，在这些加速器上部署自定义模型仍然不切实际。我们以NVIDIA Jetson DLA核心作为代表性平台。我们提出了一套五步方法论，用于零GPU回退的DLA INT8分类骨干网络部署，包括架构适配、手动动态范围变通方案以挽救TensorRT的隐式量化（从隐式量化的75%恢复94.0%准确率）从而在显式量化之前快速验证流水线、量化感知训练、面向DLA编译的ONNX图手术，以及并发的GPU检测/DLA分类推理流水线。我们记录了九个工程约束及其根因分析和可泛化的解决方案。在Jetson Orin NX上，双头人物属性分类器在DLA上运行的验证实验表明，与GPU目标检测器并发时，流水线开销接近为零（1080p下12.5对13.3 FPS的检测器单独运行），双DLA扩展无额外成本。该方法与骨干网络无关，可泛化到任何检测-分类边缘流水线。

</details>


### [47] [VOLA: Improving Open-World Driving by VLM-Based Semantic Attribute Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11777)
*Yuchen Zhang,Yuan Gao,Sebastian Schmidt,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出将自动驾驶场景感知从类别标签转向稠密动作相关属性（7级可驶性和5级脆弱性），直接读取Qwen3.5图像token隐藏状态作为空间语义表示，配合轻量级边界感知解码器生成全分辨率属性图，无需自回归文本生成或SAM等外部掩码模型。实验表明该方法在熟悉类别上匹敌视觉分割器，在真实开放世界异常场景的脆弱性排序召回率上达到69.4%，显著优于视觉分割基线（57.1%）和提示式VLM基线（53.9%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 真实世界的驾驶是开放世界的，车辆可能遇到训练数据之外的物体（如掉落的床垫、鹿等）。仅给物体命名是不够的，系统需要知道每个区域应如何影响运动决策——能否碾过、碰撞严重程度如何。因此需要将场景感知从类别标签转向稠密的、与动作相关的属性标注。

Method: 将场景感知形式化为稠密动作相关属性预测，定义两个有序属性：7级可驶性和5级脆弱性。直接读取Qwen3.5图像token的隐藏状态作为空间语义表示，通过轻量级边界感知解码器将该粗粒度token网格转换为锐利的全分辨率属性图。整个过程无需自回归文本生成，也无需SAM等外部掩码模型。

Result: 在CARLA中构建密级属性标签进行训练，并测试向真实场景和训练中未见过的新颖障碍物的迁移。模型在熟悉类别上匹敌视觉分割器；迁移到真实开放世界异常时，脆弱性排序召回率达到69.4%，优于最佳视觉分割基线（57.1%）和最佳提示式VLM基线（53.9%）。

Conclusion: VLM图像token为将驾驶属性迁移到训练词汇之外的物体提供了有用的语义线索，证明了基于属性而非类别的场景感知是处理开放世界驾驶的有效范式。

Original Abstract: Driving in the real world is open-world: a car may encounter a fallen mattress, a deer, or other objects outside its training data. Naming them is not enough. The system must know how to treat each region: can it drive over it, and how severe would a collision be? We therefore shift scene perception from category labels to dense action-relevant attributes, where each pixel is labeled by how it should affect motion rather than by object name. We instantiate this general formulation with two ordered attributes: 7-rank drivability and 5-rank vulnerability. We read Qwen3.5 image-token hidden states directly as a spatial semantic representation. A lightweight boundary-aware decoder then turns this coarse token grid into sharp full-resolution attribute maps. The whole process requires neither autoregressive text generation nor an external mask model such as SAM. We train on dense attribute labels built in CARLA and test transfer to real scenes and to novel obstacles never seen in training. We compare with vision-only segmenters trained on the same attributes and prompted VLM segmenters. Our model matches strong vision-only segmenters on familiar categories and improves transfer to real open-world anomalies, reaching 69.4% mean vulnerability-rank recall versus 57.1% for the best vision-only baseline and 53.9% for the best prompted VLM baseline. These results show that VLM image tokens provide useful semantic cues for transferring driving attributes to objects outside the training vocabulary.

中文摘要翻译: 现实世界中的驾驶是开放世界的：车辆可能遇到掉落的床垫、鹿或其他训练数据之外的物体。仅给它们命名是不够的。系统必须知道如何处理每个区域：能否碾过它，碰撞会有多严重？因此，我们将场景感知从类别标签转向稠密的、与动作相关的属性，每个像素按其应如何影响运动来标注，而非按物体名称标注。我们将这一通用形式化具体化为两个有序属性：7级可驶性和5级脆弱性。我们直接读取Qwen3.5图像token的隐藏状态作为空间语义表示。一个轻量级边界感知解码器随后将该粗粒度token网格转换为锐利的全分辨率属性图。整个过程既不需要自回归文本生成，也不需要SAM等外部掩码模型。我们在CARLA中构建的密级属性标签上训练，并测试向真实场景及训练中从未见过的新颖障碍物的迁移能力。我们将模型与在相同属性上训练的纯视觉分割器以及提示式VLM分割器进行比较。我们的模型在熟悉类别上匹敌强视觉分割器，并改善了向真实开放世界异常的迁移，平均脆弱性排序召回率达到69.4%，而最佳纯视觉基线为57.1%，最佳提示式VLM基线为53.9%。这些结果表明，VLM图像token为将驾驶属性迁移到训练词汇之外的物体提供了有用的语义线索。

</details>


### [48] [Anti-Shortcut Distillation via Temporal Negative Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11789)
*Syed Muhammad Raza,Omer Tariq,Jeongbae Son*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出反捷径蒸馏（ASD），利用教师模型优化轨迹中早期与收敛阶段特征位移的方向信息，将收敛教师作为正语义锚点、早停检查点教师作为时间负参考，通过时间对比损失和捷径抑制损失实现推-拉式知识蒸馏，在多个数据集和师生对组合上优于标准KD。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏只引导学生向收敛教师靠近，却忽略了教师自身在学习过程中学会抑制哪些方向。论文观察到：早期教师强调但收敛教师衰减的特征方向正是需要学生避免的捷径方向，这一信号已隐含在教师优化轨迹中，但现有KD方法均未利用。

Method: ASD包含两个耦合损失：(1) 时间对比损失（Ltc），将早期教师特征作为同样本负样本，与批内及记忆库中的最终教师特征构造InfoNCE目标；(2) 捷径抑制损失（Lss），惩罚学生投影到早期到最终特征位移的无中心二阶矩矩阵E[DhDh^T]的顶部特征向量方向上。整体构成推-拉式KD框架。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、ImageNet-100和TinyImageNet上共13个师生对中，ASD在超过10对上取得最高clean top-1准确率，在12对上优于标准KD；在CIFAR-100-C损坏鲁棒性上，跨架构对WRN-40-2→ShuffleNet-V2上取得最低mCE（86.1）；机制诊断显示ASD学生与捷径方向系统性反对齐，且投影到鲁棒子空间的分量显著更大（0.45 vs 0.12）。

Conclusion: 利用教师优化轨迹中的时间信息可以识别并抑制捷径方向，ASD验证了这种时间负参考机制能同时提升学生的干净准确率和鲁棒性，证明了"教师抑制的方向"是可蒸馏的有效监督信号。

Original Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) trains a compact student by attracting it towards a converged teacher. It is silent about which directions the teacher itself learned to suppress: repulsive and bias-aware objectives exist, but none exploits the teacher's own trajectory to identify what the student should avoid. We observe that the missing signal is already encoded in the teacher's optimization trajectory: features that an early-stage teacher emphasizes but that a converged teacher attenuates are precisely the shortcut directions worth pushing the student away from. We instantiate this observation as \textbf{A}nti-\textbf{S}hortcut \textbf{D}istillation (ASD), a push--pull KD framework that treats the converged teacher $\Tfinal$ as a positive semantic anchor and an early-checkpoint teacher $\Tearly$ as a temporal negative reference. ASD couples two losses: a temporal contrastive loss ($\Ltc$) that places the early-teacher feature as a same-sample negative against in-batch and memory-bank final-teacher features in an InfoNCE objective; and a shortcut suppression loss ($\Lss$) that penalizes student projection onto the top eigenvectors of $\E[\Dh\Dh^{\top}]$, the uncentered second-moment matrix of early-to-final feature displacements. Across 13 teacher--student pairs on CIFAR-100, ImageNet-100, and TinyImageNet, ASD attains the highest clean top-1 accuracy on more than 10 pairs and outperforms standard KD on 12. On CIFAR-100-C corruption robustness, ASD obtains the lowest mean Corruption Error ($86.1$\,mCE) on the most challenging cross-architecture pair (WRN-40-2$\to$ShuffleNet-V2). Mechanistic diagnostics confirm the intended geometry: the ASD student is systematically anti-aligned with the shortcut direction, while its projection onto the robust subspace is substantially larger ($0.45$ vs.\ $0.12$).

中文摘要翻译: 知识蒸馏（KD）通过吸引紧凑的学生模型向收敛的教师模型靠近来训练学生。现有方法对教师自身学会了抑制哪些方向保持沉默：虽然存在排斥性和偏置感知的目标，但没有任何方法利用教师自身的优化轨迹来识别学生应当避免什么。我们观察到这一缺失的信号已编码在教师的优化轨迹中：早期阶段教师强调但收敛教师衰减的特征，正是值得将学生推离的捷径方向。我们将这一观察实例化为反捷径蒸馏（ASD），一种推-拉式KD框架，将收敛教师Tfinal视为正语义锚点，将早期检查点教师Tearly视为时间负参考。ASD耦合两个损失：时间对比损失（Ltc），在InfoNCE目标中将早期教师特征作为同样本负样本，与批内及记忆库中的最终教师特征相对抗；以及捷径抑制损失（Lss），惩罚学生投影到E[DhDh^T]（早期到最终特征位移的无中心二阶矩矩阵）的顶部特征向量上。在CIFAR-100、ImageNet-100和TinyImageNet的13个师生对上，ASD在超过10对上取得最高干净top-1准确率，并在12对上优于标准KD。在CIFAR-100-C损坏鲁棒性上，ASD在最具挑战性的跨架构对（WRN-40-2→ShuffleNet-V2）上取得最低平均损坏误差（86.1 mCE）。机制诊断证实了预期的几何特性：ASD学生与捷径方向系统性反对齐，而其投影到鲁棒子空间的成分显著更大（0.45对0.12）。

</details>


### [49] [PolarSym: Polar Geometry-aware Attention for CAD Floorplan Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11793)
*Kerui Chen,Yiqing Wang,Kangzhou Xin,Qinghan Zhang,Songyang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PolarSym，一种基于极坐标的几何感知注意力框架，用于CAD图纸解析任务。该方法将建筑布局的几何关系解耦为方向和距离两个互补分量分别建模，并通过动态门控机制融合，在保持原始Transformer架构的同时提升了几何建模能力，在公开数据集上超越SymPoint V2基线1.73% PQ、1.54% RQ和4.31% mIoU，且计算开销可忽略不计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于Transformer的CAD平面图解析方法虽然能通过自注意力捕获全局语义依赖，但仅从语义特征推断空间关系，没有显式建模建筑布局内在的几何对称性，导致在长距离匹配和复杂对称空间布局中出现对应关系错配的问题。

Method: 提出PolarSym极坐标几何感知注意力框架：将建筑几何关系解耦为方向和距离两个互补分量并独立建模——方向约束增强结构一致性，距离约束建立长距离对称对应；通过动态门控机制协同融合两个几何信息分支，同时保持原始Transformer架构不变，以极少的额外计算提升几何建模能力。

Result: 在公开CAD平面图解析数据集上，与同等训练设置下复现的SymPoint V2基线相比，PolarSym在PQ上提升1.73%，RQ上提升1.54%，mIoU上提升4.31%；同时收敛更快、优化更稳定。消融实验验证了方向与距离建模的互补效应。

Conclusion: PolarSym以低计算成本显著提升了Transformer在CAD平面图解析中的几何感知能力，为建筑布局几何建模提供了一种有效的范式。

Original Abstract: CAD plan parsing is a fundamental task in Building Information Modeling (BIM), aiming to automatically extract architectural elements including walls, doors, windows, and furniture from 2D engineering drawings. Existing Transformer-based methods capture global semantic dependencies via self-attention, yet they infer spatial relationships merely from semantic features without explicitly characterizing the intrinsic geometric symmetry of building layouts. Such methods tend to produce mismatched correspondences in long-range matching and complex symmetric spatial layouts. To tackle this limitation, we propose PolarSym, a polar-coordinate geometry-aware attention framework for CAD plan parsing. The framework decouples geometric relationships of buildings into two complementary components, direction and distance, which are modeled independently. Structural consistency is strengthened by directional constraints, while long-range symmetric correspondences are built with distance constraints. A dynamic gating mechanism is adopted to synergistically fuse the two geometric information branches while maintaining the vanilla Transformer architecture. This design boosts geometric modeling capacity with negligible extra computation. Experiments on a public CAD plan parsing dataset show that PolarSym surpasses the reproduced SymPoint V2 baseline by 1.73% PQ, 1.54% RQ and 4.31% mIoU under identical training settings. PolarSym also converges faster and yields more stable optimization. Ablation experiments verify the complementary effects of direction and distance modeling. Our results reveal that PolarSym improves the geometric awareness of Transformers at low computational cost, offering an effective geometric modeling paradigm for CAD plan parsing.

中文摘要翻译: CAD平面图解析是建筑信息建模（BIM）中的一项基础任务，旨在从二维工程图纸中自动提取包括墙体、门、窗和家具在内的建筑构件。现有的基于Transformer的方法通过自注意力捕获全局语义依赖，然而它们仅从语义特征中推断空间关系，而没有显式刻画建筑布局内在的几何对称性。此类方法在长距离匹配和复杂对称空间布局中往往产生不匹配的对应关系。为克服这一局限，我们提出PolarSym，一种面向CAD平面图解析的极坐标几何感知注意力框架。该框架将建筑的几何关系解耦为方向和距离两个互补分量，并分别独立建模。方向约束强化结构一致性，距离约束建立长距离对称对应关系。采用动态门控机制协同融合两个几何信息分支，同时保持原始Transformer架构不变。这一设计以可忽略的额外计算代价提升了几何建模能力。在公开CAD平面图解析数据集上的实验表明，在相同训练设置下，PolarSym在PQ、RQ和mIoU上分别超越复现的SymPoint V2基线1.73%、1.54%和4.31%。PolarSym还收敛更快，优化更稳定。消融实验验证了方向建模与距离建模的互补效应。我们的结果表明，PolarSym以较低的计算成本提升了Transformer的几何感知能力，为CAD平面图解析提供了一种有效的几何建模范式。

</details>


### [50] [CoDiR: Confidence-Guided Diffusion Refinement for Semi-Supervised Histopathology Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11807)
*Hoai Nhan Pham,Dang-Nguyen Bui,Le-Van Thai,Thanh-Hiep Vo,Lan Anh Dinh Thi,Tien Dat Nguyen,Duy-Dong Nguyen,Ngoc Lam Quang Bui,Tam Tran,Zhi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoDiR框架，结合Mean Teacher分割模型与条件扩散模型，仅对低置信度区域进行伪标签精炼，在GlaS和CRAG数据集上以10%和20%标注数据显著提升半监督组织病理学分割性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 半监督组织病理学图像分割面临两大挑战：标注样本稀缺，以及模糊腺体区域产生不可靠的伪标签。现有方法未能有效处理低置信度区域中的错误伪标签，限制了分割性能。

Method: CoDiR框架包含三个核心组件：(1) Mean Teacher分割模型产生软预测；(2) 条件扩散模型仅对低置信度区域进行伪标签精炼，该扩散模型从标注数据中学习生成合理的掩膜结构；(3) 将精炼后的掩膜与可靠的教师预测融合，通过置信度加权和一致性正则化训练学生模型。

Result: 在GlaS和CRAG数据集上，CoDiR在10%标注数据下分别达到88.09%和89.83%的mDice，在20%标注下达到89.19%和90.29%，在八项基准指标中七项匹配或超过最强已发表方法。消融实验表明精炼模块贡献最大，相比Mean Teacher基线提升+6.36% mDice。

Conclusion: CoDiR通过针对性精炼低置信度区域而非整体伪标签，有效提升了半监督组织病理学分割的伪标签质量，在多个基准上达到或超越现有最优方法，验证了扩散模型用于伪标签精炼的可行性。

Original Abstract: Semi-supervised histopathology segmentation is challenging due to scarce annotations and unreliable pseudo-labels in ambiguous gland regions. To address this problem, we propose Confidence-Guided Diffusion Refinement (CoDiR), a semi-supervised framework that combines a Mean Teacher segmentation model with diffusion-based pseudo-label refinement. Given an unlabeled image, the teacher first produces a soft prediction, and only low-confidence regions are refined by a conditional diffusion model trained to capture plausible mask structures from labeled data. The refined mask is then fused with reliable teacher predictions and used to train the student with confidence weighting and consistency regularization. On the GlaS and CRAG datasets CoDiR reaches 88.09\% and 89.83\% mDice with 10\% labeled data, and 89.19\% and 90.29\% mDice with 20\%, matching or exceeding the strongest published method on seven of the eight benchmark metrics. Ablations attribute the largest single contribution to the refinement module, which adds +6.36\% mDice over the Mean Teacher baseline. The implementation code is publicly available at: https://github.com/vongla345/codir

中文摘要翻译: 半监督组织病理学分割因标注稀缺以及模糊腺体区域中不可靠的伪标签而具有挑战性。为解决这一问题，我们提出了置信度引导的扩散精炼框架（CoDiR），该框架将Mean Teacher分割模型与基于扩散的伪标签精炼相结合。给定一张无标签图像，教师模型首先生成软预测，仅对低置信度区域通过条件扩散模型进行精炼，该扩散模型从标注数据中学习捕捉合理的掩膜结构。随后将精炼后的掩膜与可靠的教师预测相融合，并通过置信度加权和一致性正则化用于训练学生模型。在GlaS和CRAG数据集上，CoDiR在10%标注数据下达到88.09%和89.83%的mDice，在20%标注数据下达到89.19%和90.29%的mDice，在八项基准指标中有七项达到或超过最强已发表方法。消融实验表明最大的单项贡献来自精炼模块，相比Mean Teacher基线提升了+6.36%的mDice。实现代码公开于：https://github.com/vongla345/codir

</details>


### [51] [Can Vision Models Read the Radar Display? On the Feasibility of Radar Imagery for Air Traffic Complexity Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11810)
*Hyewook Kim,Byul Kang,Seokbin Yoon,Keumjin Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文验证了雷达图像作为深度学习视觉模型输入来建模空中交通管制员感知的复杂性的可行性，使用Vision Transformer（ViT）对由飞机位置图像及五个状态变量通道组成的输入进行回归，预测四个内在复杂度分量，取得R²>0.96的成绩，并通过单机移除扰动实验证明模型响应与飞机的实际贡献成正比。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 空中交通管制员通过雷达显示器感知交通复杂度，因此基于相同图像输入的计算机视觉模型可能是建模管制员感知复杂度的自然架构。然而，雷达图像不同于自然图像——极其稀疏且自相似，主要由黑色背景和少数视觉相同的飞机目标组成，而飞机位置的微小变化可能显著改变扇区级复杂度。因此需要验证视觉模型能否捕捉这些操作性重要的差异。

Method: 将每个交通态势编码为一张位置图像，并附加五个通道表示飞机状态变量（航向、速度、高度等），共计多通道输入。使用Vision Transformer（ViT）回归由飞机两两几何关系导出的四个内在复杂度分量。

Result: 模型在四个复杂度分量上均取得R²>0.96。单机移除扰动研究表明，模型响应变化与移除飞机对扇区复杂度的实际贡献成比例，而非将所有移除视为等价，说明模型学到了操作上有意义的复杂度差异。

Conclusion: 尽管雷达图像具有非典型的视觉特征，它仍然是空中交通复杂度建模的可行输入格式，计算机视觉模型能够有效从雷达图像中提取管制员感知的复杂度信息。

Original Abstract: Air traffic controllers perceive traffic complexity through the radar display, suggesting that a computer vision model operating on the same imagery may provide a natural architecture for modeling controller-perceived complexity; however, whether radar imagery is a viable input format for deep learning vision models remains unclear. Unlike natural images, radar images are extremely sparse and self-similar, consisting primarily of a black background and a few visually identical aircraft blobs, while small changes in aircraft positions can substantially alter sector-level complexity. To test whether a vision model can capture these operationally important differences, we encode each traffic situation as a position image supplemented by five channels representing aircraft state variables, including heading, speed, and altitude, and train a Vision Transformer (ViT) to regress four intrinsic complexity components derived from pairwise geometric relations among aircraft. The model achieves $R^2 > 0.96$ for all four components, and a one-aircraft-removal perturbation study shows that its response changes proportionally to how much the removed aircraft contributed to sector complexity rather than treating every removal as equivalent. These results demonstrate that, despite its atypical visual characteristics, radar imagery is a viable input format for air traffic complexity modeling.

中文摘要翻译: 空中交通管制员通过雷达显示器感知交通复杂度，这表明基于相同图像运行的计算机视觉模型可能为建模管制员感知的复杂度提供一种自然的架构；然而，雷达图像是否是深度学习视觉模型的可行输入格式仍不清楚。与自然图像不同，雷达图像极度稀疏且自相似，主要由黑色背景和少数视觉上相同的飞机光点组成，而飞机位置的微小变化可能显著改变扇区级复杂度。为了检验视觉模型能否捕捉这些在操作上重要的差异，我们将每个交通态势编码为一张位置图像，并附加五个表示飞机状态变量（包括航向、速度和高度）的通道，训练一个Vision Transformer（ViT）来回归由飞机间两两几何关系导出的四个内在复杂度分量。该模型在全部四个分量上均达到R²>0.96，且单机移除扰动研究表明，其响应变化与移除飞机对扇区复杂度的贡献成比例，而非将所有移除视为等价。这些结果表明，尽管雷达图像具有非典型的视觉特征，它仍是空中交通复杂度建模的可行输入格式。

</details>


### [52] [TD-VAD: Breaking Visual Dependence in Video Anomaly Detection with Text-Driven Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11820)
*Shuangqing Zhang,Lei-Lei Ma,Zhao Wang,Wen Dong,Xinyi Xu,Guo-Sen Xie,Caifeng Shan,Fang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需目标域异常视频数据的文本驱动视频异常检测方法（TD-VAD），利用LLM生成的类视频时序文本描述训练VAD模型，并通过事件演化因果注意力模块建模上下文依赖，推理时使用冻结CLIP编码器对齐文本与视频模态。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频异常检测（VAD）方法严重依赖视觉异常数据，而异常数据稀缺且种类繁多，收集充分标注的数据难以扩展。相比之下，异常事件的文本描述易于获取且类标签可直接推导，因此作者主张将文本作为视频序列的替代输入来训练VAD模型，打破对视觉数据的依赖。

Method: 提出TD-VAD方法：1）利用LLM生成具有时间特征的类视频文本描述来训练VAD模型，完全不依赖目标域异常数据；2）设计事件演化因果注意力模块（Event Evolution Causal Attention Module）来建模长程和短程的时间逻辑，捕获事件跨时间的上下文依赖；3）推理阶段使用冻结的CLIP编码器提取视频帧的嵌入，对齐文本模态的同时保留关键视觉信息，以缓解文本与视频之间的域差距。

Result: 在XD-Violence和UCF-Crime两个大规模VAD数据集上的综合实验表明，该方法大幅优于先前的一类（one-class）和无监督（unsupervised）VAD方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用文本描述的易获取性和LLM的生成能力，可以有效地训练视频异常检测模型而无需依赖目标域的异常视频数据，且能取得优于传统监督依赖方法的检测性能。

Original Abstract: Visual data is typically a prerequisite for training existing video anomaly detection (VAD) methods. However, obtaining sufficient annotated anomaly data for training is challenging and not scalable due to the rarity of anomaly data and the wide variety of abnormal events. In this work, we advocate that the effectiveness of treating texts as video sequences for the VAD model and propose a novel Text-Driven Video Anomaly Detection (TD-VAD) approach to break visual dependence. In contrast to the anomaly video data, text descriptions of abnormal events are easy to collect, and their class labels can be directly derived. Specifically, our method utilizes video-like text descriptions with temporal characteristics generated by LLM to train a VAD model, without any reliance on target-domain anomaly data. To capture the long- and short-range temporal logic of events, we design the event evolution causal attention module to model contextual dependencies across time. During inference, considering the domain gap between the texts and video sequences, we use the frozen CLIP encoder to extract embeddings of video frames to align the text modality while retaining crucial visual information. Comprehensive experiments on two large-scale VAD datasets, XD-Violence and UCF-Crime, demonstrate that our method outperforms prior one-class and unsupervised VAD methods by a large margin.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉数据通常是训练现有视频异常检测（VAD）方法的先决条件。然而，由于异常数据的稀缺性和异常事件的多样性，获取充足的标注异常数据进行训练具有挑战性且难以扩展。在这项工作中，我们主张将文本视为视频序列用于VAD模型的有效性，并提出一种新颖的文本驱动视频异常检测（TD-VAD）方法来打破视觉依赖。与异常视频数据不同，异常事件的文本描述易于收集，且其类标签可以直接推导。具体而言，我们的方法利用LLM生成的具有时间特征的类视频文本描述来训练VAD模型，完全不依赖目标域的异常数据。为了捕获事件的长程和短程时间逻辑，我们设计了事件演化因果注意力模块来建模跨时间的上下文依赖。在推理阶段，考虑到文本与视频序列之间的域差距，我们使用冻结的CLIP编码器提取视频帧的嵌入，以对齐文本模态，同时保留关键视觉信息。在两个大规模VAD数据集XD-Violence和UCF-Crime上的综合实验表明，我们的方法大幅优于先前的一类（one-class）和无监督（unsupervised）VAD方法。

</details>


### [53] [Distractor-Aware Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11835)
*Andreas Robinson,Abdelrahman Eldesokey,Michael Felsberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种半监督视频目标分割的新方案——一对一多（one-versus-many）分类架构，将干扰物（distractors）从背景中分离并单独作为一类进行处理，通过对学习范式（LWL）方法的改进使其具有干扰物感知能力，在DAVIS 2017数据集上取得了新的最优结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有判别式方法通常将视频分割问题建模为目标与背景之间的一对一（one-versus-one）分类。然而真实视频中除了目标和背景外，往往还包含与目标视觉相似的干扰物，这些干扰物容易引发误报（false positives），降低分割性能。因此有必要将干扰物从背景中分离出来并单独处理。

Method: 提出一对多（one-versus-many）分类方案，将干扰物划分为独立的类别，使其与背景分离。该分离机制能够对最可能导致性能退化的困难区域施加特殊关注。作者通过将学习什么（learning-what-to-learn, LWL）方法修改为具有干扰物感知（distractor-aware）能力来实现这一方案。

Result: 在DAVIS 2017 val数据集上取得了新的最优性能（state-of-the-art），并在DAVIS 2017 test-dev基准上相比基线方法提升了4.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 将干扰物作为独立类别进行显式建模的one-versus-many方案能够有效降低误报风险，提升半监督视频目标分割的准确性和鲁棒性，验证了该公式化方法的有效性。

Original Abstract: Semi-supervised video object segmentation is a challenging task that aims to segment a target throughout a video sequence given an initial mask at the first frame. Discriminative approaches have demonstrated competitive performance on this task at a sensible complexity. These approaches typically formulate the problem as a one-versus-one classification between the target and the background. However, in reality, a video sequence usually encompasses a target, background, and possibly other distracting objects. Those objects increase the risk of introducing false positives, especially if they share visual similarities with the target. Therefore, it is more effective to separate distractors from the background, and handle them independently.
  We propose a one-versus-many scheme to address this situation by separating distractors into their own class. This separation allows imposing special attention to challenging regions that are most likely to degrade the performance. We demonstrate the prominence of this formulation by modifying the learning-what-to-learn (LWL) method to be distractor-aware. Our proposed approach sets a new state-of-the-art on the DAVIS 2017 val dataset, and improves over the baseline on the DAVIS 2017 test-dev benchmark by 4.6 percentage points.

中文摘要翻译: 半监督视频目标分割是一项具有挑战性的任务，其目标是在给定首帧初始掩码的情况下，在整段视频序列中分割出目标对象。判别式方法在此任务上以合理的复杂度展现了具有竞争力的性能。这类方法通常将问题建模为目标与背景之间的一对一分类。然而，现实中的视频序列通常包含目标、背景，以及可能存在的其他干扰物体。这些物体增加了引入误报的风险，尤其是当它们与目标共享视觉相似性时。因此，将干扰物与背景分离并独立处理更为有效。我们提出了一种一对多方案来处理这一情况，将干扰物划分为独立的类别。这种分离使得能够对最可能导致性能退化的困难区域施加特殊关注。我们通过将学习什么学习（learning-what-to-learn, LWL）方法修改为具有干扰物感知能力来证明该公式化的优越性。我们提出的方法在DAVIS 2017 val数据集上取得了新的最优性能，并在DAVIS 2017 test-dev基准上相比基线提升了4.6个百分点。

</details>


### [54] [GeoBridge: Decoupled Semantic Conditioning for Generative Image Geolocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11838)
*Zhiyang Dou,Xumeng Han,Fengde Peng,Zipeng Wang,Moxuan Zhao,Zhipei Huang,Zhenjun Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoBridge提出一种角色解耦的条件生成机制，将冻结的语义多模态大语言模型与冻结的黎曼流形匹配头连接，直接在球面上生成图像地理定位坐标，解决了语义监督与连续几何生成之间的角色冲突，在IM2GPS3K基准上显著提升定位精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)在图像地理定位方面主要改进其地理线索推理能力，但如何将推理解码为坐标的方式进展滞后。现有的"预测地名并通过地理编码API转换"方式是有损且离散的，忽略了图像证据并将多粒度语义压缩为粗糙查询。作者认为瓶颈已从模型推理内容转移到推理如何在连续几何感知解码器中表示。

Method: GeoBridge采用角色解耦的条件生成机制：将冻结的语义MLLM连接到冻结的黎曼流形匹配头（Riemannian flow-matching head），在球面上生成坐标。核心创新在于将语义监督与条件接口解耦——使用独立的投影层形成连续条件输入给冻结的生成头，注入地理先验而不干扰球面解码器，从而解决语义标签监督偏置条件表示朝向类别判别性几何、与生成头所需光滑流形不一致的角色冲突问题。

Result: 在IM2GPS3K基准上，GeoBridge在25/200/750 km阈值下分别达到38.67/52.89/70.37的精度，在精度相关尺度上优于地名到API流水线和增强推理的直接预测方法。

Conclusion: GeoBridge是解码端的算法贡献，与思维链推理正交且互补，证明了地理定位瓶颈在于推理的表示方式而非推理内容本身。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced image geolocalization mainly by improving how they reason about geographic cues. How that reasoning isdecoded into coordinates, however, has lagged behind. Predicting a place name for a geocoding API is discrete and lossy: it ignores image evidence and collapses multi-granular semantics into a coarse lookup. We argue that the bottleneck has shifted from what a model reasons to how that reasoning is represented for a continuous, geometry-aware decoder. We present GeoBridge, a role-decoupled conditioning mechanism that connects a frozen semantic MLLM to a frozen Riemannian flow-matching head that generates coordinates on the sphere. The central obstacle is arole conflict: supervising the condition with discrete semantic labels biases its representation toward class-discriminative geometry, at odds with the smooth manifold the generative head requires. GeoBridge keeps the semantic supervision decoupled from the condition interface: a separate projection forms the continuous condition the frozen head expects, injecting geographic priors without disturbing the spherical decoder.
